import '../contributions/authenticity_contribution.dart';
import '../contributions/contribution_conflict_policy.dart';
import '../contributions/visual_inspection_contribution.dart';
import '../engine/abstract_scan_engine.dart';
import '../engine/engine_context.dart';
import '../engine/engine_result.dart';
import '../engine/engine_support.dart';
import '../models/scan_session.dart';
import 'atlas_authenticity_rules.dart';

final class AtlasAuthenticityEngine extends AbstractScanEngine {
  const AtlasAuthenticityEngine();

  static const id = 'atlas.scan.authenticity';
  static const capabilityId = 'atlas.capability.authenticity';

  @override
  String get engineId => id;

  @override
  String get displayName => 'Authenticity Intelligence';

  @override
  Future<EngineResult> execute(
    ScanSession session,
    EngineContext context,
  ) async {
    final visual = session
        .latestContributionOfType<VisualInspectionContribution>();
    if (visual == null) {
      return EngineResult.skipped(
        session: session,
        reason: 'No visual inspection evidence is available.',
      );
    }

    final assessment = AtlasAuthenticityRules.evaluate(
      session: session,
      inspection: visual.inspection,
    );

    final contribution = AuthenticityContribution(
      contributionId: EngineSupport.buildContributionId(
        namespace: 'atlas.authenticity',
        segments: <Object?>[session.sessionId],
      ),
      sourceEngineId: engineId,
      createdAt: DateTime.now().toUtc(),
      assessment: assessment,
      confidence: assessment.evidenceConfidence,
      attributes: <String, Object?>{
        'status': assessment.status.name,
        'concernLevel': assessment.concernLevel.name,
        'evidenceConfidence': assessment.evidenceConfidence,
        'referenceCoverage': assessment.referenceCoverage,
        'inspectionCoverage': assessment.inspectionCoverage,
        'concernScore': assessment.aggregatedConcernScore,
      },
    );

    final updated = session.addContribution(
      contribution,
      conflictPolicy: ContributionConflictPolicy.replaceExisting,
    );

    return EngineResult.success(session: updated);
  }
}

import '../../intelligence/identity/atlas_identity_analysis_engine.dart';
import '../models/scan_session.dart';
import 'atlas_production_stamp_parser.dart';
import 'models/atlas_authenticity_assessment.dart';
import 'models/atlas_authenticity_evidence.dart';
import 'models/atlas_authenticity_signal.dart';
import 'models/atlas_inspection_reference.dart';
import 'models/atlas_production_stamp.dart';
import 'models/atlas_visual_inspection.dart';

abstract final class AtlasAuthenticityRules {
  static const double minimumEvidenceConfidence = 0.40;
  static const double categoryConcernCap = 0.35;
  static const double reviewThreshold = 0.10;
  static const double significantThreshold = 0.35;
  static const double strongIndicatorThreshold = 0.65;

  static AtlasAuthenticityAssessment evaluate({
    required ScanSession session,
    required AtlasVisualInspection inspection,
  }) {
    final List<AtlasAuthenticitySignal> displaySignals =
        <AtlasAuthenticitySignal>[];
    final List<AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal> evaluatedSignals =
        <AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal>[];
    final List<String> audit = <String>[];

    final AtlasInspectionReferenceCriteria reference =
        inspection.referenceCriteria ?? _fallbackReference(session);

    _evaluateLegacyInspection(
      session: session,
      inspection: inspection,
      reference: reference,
      displaySignals: displaySignals,
      evaluatedSignals: evaluatedSignals,
    );

    _evaluateStructuredObservations(
      inspection: inspection,
      reference: reference,
      evaluatedSignals: evaluatedSignals,
      displaySignals: displaySignals,
    );

    final double evidenceConfidence = inspection.resolvedEvidenceConfidence;
    final double inspectionCoverage =
        inspection.inspectedAreas.length / AtlasInspectionArea.values.length;
    final List<AtlasInspectionArea> uninspectedAreas = AtlasInspectionArea
        .values
        .where(
          (AtlasInspectionArea area) =>
              !inspection.inspectedAreas.contains(area),
        )
        .toList(growable: false);

    if (!inspection.hasAnyLegacyEvidence &&
        !inspection.hasStructuredObservations) {
      audit.add(
        'No inspection evidence was supplied. Uninspected areas do not count as discrepancies.',
      );
      return _assessment(
        concernLevel: AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.insufficientData,
        evidenceConfidence: 0.0,
        referenceCoverage: reference.completenessScore,
        inspectionCoverage: 0.0,
        aggregatedConcernScore: 0.0,
        summary:
            'There is not enough evidence to form a useful authenticity assessment.',
        displaySignals: displaySignals,
        evaluatedSignals: evaluatedSignals,
        uninspectedAreas: uninspectedAreas,
        audit: audit,
        productionStamp: inspection.hasProductionEvidence
            ? AtlasProductionStampParser.parse(inspection.productionStampText)
            : null,
      );
    }

    if (evidenceConfidence < minimumEvidenceConfidence) {
      audit.add(
        'Evidence confidence is below the assessment threshold. Low-quality evidence is not treated as counterfeit evidence.',
      );
      return _assessment(
        concernLevel: AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.insufficientData,
        evidenceConfidence: evidenceConfidence,
        referenceCoverage: reference.completenessScore,
        inspectionCoverage: inspectionCoverage,
        aggregatedConcernScore: 0.0,
        summary:
            'The available images or observations are not clear enough for a reliable authenticity comparison.',
        displaySignals: displaySignals,
        evaluatedSignals: evaluatedSignals,
        uninspectedAreas: uninspectedAreas,
        audit: audit,
        productionStamp: inspection.hasProductionEvidence
            ? AtlasProductionStampParser.parse(inspection.productionStampText)
            : null,
      );
    }

    if (!reference.canSupportConcernAssessment) {
      audit.add(
        'Reference coverage is insufficient for concern scoring. Incomplete reference data is not treated as counterfeit evidence.',
      );
      return _assessment(
        concernLevel: AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.insufficientData,
        evidenceConfidence: evidenceConfidence,
        referenceCoverage: reference.completenessScore,
        inspectionCoverage: inspectionCoverage,
        aggregatedConcernScore: 0.0,
        summary:
            'Atlas recorded the inspection evidence, but the reference data is not complete enough for a reliable authenticity comparison.',
        displaySignals: displaySignals,
        evaluatedSignals: evaluatedSignals,
        uninspectedAreas: uninspectedAreas,
        audit: audit,
        productionStamp: inspection.hasProductionEvidence
            ? AtlasProductionStampParser.parse(inspection.productionStampText)
            : null,
      );
    }

    final _ConcernAggregation aggregation = _aggregateConcern(
      evaluatedSignals,
      audit,
    );

    final AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel concernLevel;
    if (aggregation.criticalCategories >= 2 &&
        aggregation.score >= strongIndicatorThreshold) {
      concernLevel = AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.strongCounterfeitIndicators;
      audit.add(
        'Strong indicators require critical contradictions in at least two independent evidence categories.',
      );
    } else if (aggregation.score >= significantThreshold) {
      concernLevel = AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.significantInconsistencies;
    } else if (aggregation.score > reviewThreshold) {
      concernLevel = AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.reviewRecommended;
    } else {
      concernLevel = AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.noObviousConcerns;
    }

    return _assessment(
      concernLevel: concernLevel,
      evidenceConfidence: evidenceConfidence,
      referenceCoverage: reference.completenessScore,
      inspectionCoverage: inspectionCoverage,
      aggregatedConcernScore: aggregation.score,
      summary: _summaryForConcern(concernLevel),
      displaySignals: displaySignals,
      evaluatedSignals: evaluatedSignals,
      uninspectedAreas: uninspectedAreas,
      audit: audit,
      productionStamp: inspection.hasProductionEvidence
          ? AtlasProductionStampParser.parse(inspection.productionStampText)
          : null,
    );
  }

  static void _evaluateLegacyInspection({
    required ScanSession session,
    required AtlasVisualInspection inspection,
    required AtlasInspectionReferenceCriteria reference,
    required List<AtlasAuthenticitySignal> displaySignals,
    required List<AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal> evaluatedSignals,
  }) {
    final bool hasStructuredVariantSticker = inspection.observations.any(
      (AtlasVisualEvidenceObservation observation) =>
          observation.category == AtlasEvidenceCategory.variantSticker,
    );
    final bool hasStructuredProductionStamp = inspection.observations.any(
      (AtlasVisualEvidenceObservation observation) =>
          observation.category == AtlasEvidenceCategory.productionStamp,
    );
    final bool hasStructuredMacroPackaging = inspection.observations.any(
      (AtlasVisualEvidenceObservation observation) =>
          observation.category == AtlasEvidenceCategory.macroPackaging,
    );
    final bool hasStructuredAutographCoa = inspection.observations.any(
      (AtlasVisualEvidenceObservation observation) =>
          observation.category == AtlasEvidenceCategory.autographCoa,
    );

    if (inspection.hasStickerEvidence && !hasStructuredVariantSticker) {
      final analysis = AtlasIdentityAnalysisEngine.create().analyze(
        inspection.stickerText,
      );
      final Set<String> detected = <String>{
        ...analysis.stickerResult.stickers.map((value) => value.displayName),
        ...analysis.variantResult.variants.map((value) => value.displayName),
      };

      if (detected.isEmpty) {
        displaySignals.add(
          const AtlasAuthenticitySignal(
            id: 'sticker-unclassified',
            title: 'Sticker needs confirmation',
            message: 'Atlas could not classify the observed sticker text.',
            level: AtlasAuthenticitySignalLevel.caution,
            weight: -5,
          ),
        );
        evaluatedSignals.add(
          AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal(
            id: 'sticker-unclassified',
            area: AtlasInspectionArea.windowSticker,
            category: AtlasEvidenceCategory.variantSticker,
            severity: AtlasSignalSeverity.advisory,
            disposition: AtlasSignalDisposition.observational,
            confidence: inspection.resolvedEvidenceConfidence,
            baseWeight: 0.0,
            rationale:
                'The sticker text could not be classified. Unknown is not a discrepancy.',
          ),
        );
      } else {
        displaySignals.add(
          AtlasAuthenticitySignal(
            id: 'sticker-classified',
            title: 'Sticker evidence classified',
            message: detected.join(' • '),
            level: AtlasAuthenticitySignalLevel.support,
            weight: 10,
          ),
        );
      }

      final String expectedVariant =
          session.resolvedProduct?.variantName?.trim() ?? '';
      if (expectedVariant.isNotEmpty &&
          !inspection.stickerText.toLowerCase().contains(
            expectedVariant.toLowerCase(),
          )) {
        displaySignals.add(
          const AtlasAuthenticitySignal(
            id: 'sticker-variant-conflict',
            title: 'Sticker and catalog variant differ',
            message:
                'Confirm the exact sticker and physical variant before saving.',
            level: AtlasAuthenticitySignalLevel.warning,
            weight: -25,
          ),
        );
        evaluatedSignals.add(
          AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal(
            id: 'sticker-variant-conflict',
            area: AtlasInspectionArea.windowSticker,
            category: AtlasEvidenceCategory.variantSticker,
            severity: AtlasSignalSeverity.moderate,
            disposition: AtlasSignalDisposition.discrepancy,
            confidence: inspection.resolvedEvidenceConfidence,
            baseWeight: 0.25,
            rationale:
                'Observed sticker text does not contain the resolved catalog variant.',
          ),
        );
      }

      if (reference.legitimateVariantTypes.isNotEmpty &&
          !reference.legitimateVariantTypes.any(
            (String variant) => inspection.stickerText.toLowerCase().contains(
              variant.toLowerCase(),
            ),
          )) {
        evaluatedSignals.add(
          AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal(
            id: 'sticker-reference-mismatch',
            area: AtlasInspectionArea.windowSticker,
            category: AtlasEvidenceCategory.variantSticker,
            severity: AtlasSignalSeverity.moderate,
            disposition: AtlasSignalDisposition.discrepancy,
            confidence: inspection.resolvedEvidenceConfidence,
            baseWeight: 0.28,
            rationale:
                'Observed sticker text does not match a recorded legitimate variant family.',
          ),
        );
      }
    }

    if (inspection.hasProductionEvidence && !hasStructuredProductionStamp) {
      final stamp = AtlasProductionStampParser.parse(
        inspection.productionStampText,
      );
      if (stamp.isValid) {
        displaySignals.add(
          AtlasAuthenticitySignal(
            id: 'production-stamp',
            title: '${stamp.factoryCode} production stamp recognized',
            message: stamp.itemBatch ?? inspection.productionStampText,
            level: AtlasAuthenticitySignalLevel.support,
            weight: 10,
          ),
        );
      } else {
        displaySignals.add(
          const AtlasAuthenticitySignal(
            id: 'production-stamp-unreadable',
            title: 'Production stamp needs review',
            message:
                'The bottom-box marking is not a recognized JJL/FAC format.',
            level: AtlasAuthenticitySignalLevel.caution,
            weight: -8,
          ),
        );
        evaluatedSignals.add(
          AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal(
            id: 'production-stamp-unreadable',
            area: AtlasInspectionArea.bottomStamp,
            category: AtlasEvidenceCategory.productionStamp,
            severity: AtlasSignalSeverity.advisory,
            disposition: AtlasSignalDisposition.observational,
            confidence: inspection.resolvedEvidenceConfidence,
            baseWeight: 0.0,
            rationale:
                'An unreadable or unsupported stamp format is insufficient evidence, not a contradiction.',
          ),
        );
      }
    }

    if (!hasStructuredVariantSticker) {
      _addLegacyCheck(
        value: inspection.stickerPrintClear,
        id: 'sticker-print',
        area: AtlasInspectionArea.windowSticker,
        category: AtlasEvidenceCategory.variantSticker,
        goodTitle: 'Sticker print appears clear',
        warningTitle: 'Sticker print needs comparison',
        warningMessage:
            'Blurred or uneven printing can warrant closer comparison.',
        displaySignals: displaySignals,
        evaluatedSignals: evaluatedSignals,
        confidence: inspection.resolvedEvidenceConfidence,
      );
      _addLegacyCheck(
        value: inspection.stickerCutEven,
        id: 'sticker-cut',
        area: AtlasInspectionArea.windowSticker,
        category: AtlasEvidenceCategory.variantSticker,
        goodTitle: 'Sticker cut appears even',
        warningTitle: 'Sticker cut needs comparison',
        warningMessage:
            'An uneven border or cut can warrant closer comparison.',
        displaySignals: displaySignals,
        evaluatedSignals: evaluatedSignals,
        confidence: inspection.resolvedEvidenceConfidence,
      );
    }

    if (!hasStructuredMacroPackaging) {
      _addLegacyCheck(
        value: inspection.typographyConsistent,
        id: 'typography',
        area: AtlasInspectionArea.packagingCopyright,
        category: AtlasEvidenceCategory.macroPackaging,
        goodTitle: 'Macro typography appears consistent',
        warningTitle: 'Packaging typography needs comparison',
        warningMessage:
            'Large typography or layout differences should be reviewed, allowing for legitimate factory variance.',
        displaySignals: displaySignals,
        evaluatedSignals: evaluatedSignals,
        confidence: inspection.resolvedEvidenceConfidence,
      );
      _addLegacyCheck(
        value: inspection.borderConsistent,
        id: 'box-border',
        area: AtlasInspectionArea.packagingCopyright,
        category: AtlasEvidenceCategory.macroPackaging,
        goodTitle: 'Macro box layout appears consistent',
        warningTitle: 'Packaging layout needs comparison',
        warningMessage:
            'Large border or layout differences should be reviewed, allowing for legitimate manufacturing variance.',
        displaySignals: displaySignals,
        evaluatedSignals: evaluatedSignals,
        confidence: inspection.resolvedEvidenceConfidence,
      );
    }

    if (inspection.hasCopyrightEvidence && !hasStructuredMacroPackaging) {
      displaySignals.add(
        const AtlasAuthenticitySignal(
          id: 'copyright',
          title: 'Copyright evidence recorded',
          message: 'Copyright text is preserved for comparison and review.',
          level: AtlasAuthenticitySignalLevel.support,
          weight: 5,
        ),
      );
    }

    if (inspection.hasAutograph == true && !hasStructuredAutographCoa) {
      if (inspection.hasCoa == true && inspection.hasAuthenticationEvidence) {
        displaySignals.add(
          const AtlasAuthenticitySignal(
            id: 'autograph-documented',
            title: 'Autograph documentation recorded',
            message:
                'A COA provider or certification number was supplied. Atlas has not independently verified it.',
            level: AtlasAuthenticitySignalLevel.support,
            weight: 10,
          ),
        );
      } else {
        displaySignals.add(
          const AtlasAuthenticitySignal(
            id: 'autograph-unverified',
            title: 'Autograph verification not supplied',
            message:
                'The autograph was recorded without provider-verification evidence. This does not mean the autograph is invalid.',
            level: AtlasAuthenticitySignalLevel.caution,
            weight: 0,
          ),
        );
      }
      evaluatedSignals.add(
        AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal(
          id: 'autograph-observation',
          area: AtlasInspectionArea.autograph,
          category: AtlasEvidenceCategory.autographCoa,
          severity: AtlasSignalSeverity.info,
          disposition: AtlasSignalDisposition.observational,
          confidence: inspection.resolvedEvidenceConfidence,
          baseWeight: 0.0,
          rationale:
              'Autograph and COA data are observational in Scanner 5A and do not independently create counterfeit concern.',
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  static void _evaluateStructuredObservations({
    required AtlasVisualInspection inspection,
    required AtlasInspectionReferenceCriteria reference,
    required List<AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal> evaluatedSignals,
    required List<AtlasAuthenticitySignal> displaySignals,
  }) {
    for (final AtlasVisualEvidenceObservation observation
        in inspection.observations) {
      switch (observation.category) {
        case AtlasEvidenceCategory.productionStamp:
          final String? prefix =
              observation.attributes['factoryPrefix'] as String?;
          final DateTime? parsedDate =
              observation.attributes['parsedDate'] as DateTime?;
          if (prefix == null ||
              parsedDate == null ||
              reference.knownRuns.isEmpty) {
            evaluatedSignals.add(
              AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal(
                id: observation.observationId,
                area: observation.area,
                category: observation.category,
                severity: AtlasSignalSeverity.info,
                disposition: AtlasSignalDisposition.observational,
                confidence: observation.confidence,
                baseWeight: 0.0,
                rationale:
                    'Production evidence was recorded without enough structured reference data for a contradiction test.',
              ),
            );
            continue;
          }

          final bool matchesKnownRun = reference.knownRuns.any(
            (AtlasProductionRunReference run) =>
                run.matchesStamp(prefix: prefix, date: parsedDate),
          );
          evaluatedSignals.add(
            AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal(
              id: observation.observationId,
              area: observation.area,
              category: observation.category,
              severity: matchesKnownRun
                  ? AtlasSignalSeverity.info
                  : AtlasSignalSeverity.critical,
              disposition: matchesKnownRun
                  ? AtlasSignalDisposition.supporting
                  : AtlasSignalDisposition.discrepancy,
              confidence: observation.confidence,
              baseWeight: matchesKnownRun ? 0.0 : 0.50,
              rationale: matchesKnownRun
                  ? 'Production code aligns with a recorded manufacturing run.'
                  : 'Production code conflicts with recorded factory/date windows.',
            ),
          );
          break;

        case AtlasEvidenceCategory.variantSticker:
          final bool explicitCriticalMismatch =
              observation.attributes['criticalReferenceMismatch'] == true;
          final bool referenceMismatch =
              observation.attributes['referenceMismatch'] == true;
          final bool visualMismatch =
              observation.attributes['visualMismatch'] == true;
          final bool resolvedVariantMismatch =
              observation.attributes['resolvedVariantMismatch'] == true;

          if (explicitCriticalMismatch ||
              referenceMismatch ||
              visualMismatch ||
              resolvedVariantMismatch) {
            final AtlasSignalSeverity severity = explicitCriticalMismatch
                ? AtlasSignalSeverity.critical
                : referenceMismatch || resolvedVariantMismatch
                ? AtlasSignalSeverity.moderate
                : AtlasSignalSeverity.advisory;
            final double baseWeight = explicitCriticalMismatch
                ? 0.50
                : referenceMismatch || resolvedVariantMismatch
                ? 0.25
                : 0.12;
            evaluatedSignals.add(
              AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal(
                id: observation.observationId,
                area: observation.area,
                category: observation.category,
                severity: severity,
                disposition: AtlasSignalDisposition.discrepancy,
                confidence: observation.confidence,
                baseWeight: baseWeight,
                rationale: explicitCriticalMismatch
                    ? 'A specialized sticker engine reported a high-signal contradiction against vetted reference evidence.'
                    : referenceMismatch
                    ? 'Observed sticker or variant evidence differs from the recorded legitimate reference families.'
                    : resolvedVariantMismatch
                    ? 'Observed sticker or variant evidence differs from the resolved catalog variant and should be confirmed.'
                    : 'A visible sticker print or cut inconsistency was recorded for collector review.',
              ),
            );
          }
          break;

        case AtlasEvidenceCategory.macroPackaging:
          final bool explicitCriticalMismatch =
              observation.attributes['criticalReferenceMismatch'] == true;
          final bool macroMismatch =
              observation.attributes['macroMismatch'] == true;
          if (explicitCriticalMismatch || macroMismatch) {
            evaluatedSignals.add(
              AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal(
                id: observation.observationId,
                area: observation.area,
                category: observation.category,
                severity: explicitCriticalMismatch
                    ? AtlasSignalSeverity.critical
                    : AtlasSignalSeverity.advisory,
                disposition: AtlasSignalDisposition.discrepancy,
                confidence: observation.confidence,
                baseWeight: explicitCriticalMismatch ? 0.50 : 0.12,
                rationale: explicitCriticalMismatch
                    ? 'A specialized packaging engine reported a high-signal contradiction against vetted reference evidence.'
                    : 'A macro packaging difference was observed and should be reviewed with manufacturing variance in mind.',
              ),
            );
          }
          break;

        case AtlasEvidenceCategory.autographCoa:
          final bool internalMismatch =
              observation.attributes['internalMismatch'] == true;
          final bool providerVerified =
              observation.attributes['providerVerified'] == true;
          final String documentationState =
              observation.attributes['documentationState'] as String? ?? '';

          if (internalMismatch) {
            displaySignals.add(
              const AtlasAuthenticitySignal(
                id: 'authentication-details-inconsistent',
                title: 'Authentication details need review',
                message:
                    'Observed COA text differs from manually recorded provider or certificate details. Confirm the document before relying on it.',
                level: AtlasAuthenticitySignalLevel.caution,
                weight: -8,
              ),
            );
          } else if (documentationState == 'supported') {
            displaySignals.add(
              const AtlasAuthenticitySignal(
                id: 'authentication-documentation-recorded',
                title: 'Authentication documentation recorded',
                message:
                    'Provider and certificate details were observed. Atlas has not independently verified the provider record.',
                level: AtlasAuthenticitySignalLevel.support,
                weight: 5,
              ),
            );
          }

          evaluatedSignals.add(
            AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal(
              id: observation.observationId,
              area: observation.area,
              category: observation.category,
              severity: internalMismatch
                  ? AtlasSignalSeverity.advisory
                  : AtlasSignalSeverity.info,
              disposition: internalMismatch
                  ? AtlasSignalDisposition.discrepancy
                  : providerVerified
                  ? AtlasSignalDisposition.supporting
                  : AtlasSignalDisposition.observational,
              confidence: observation.confidence,
              baseWeight: internalMismatch ? 0.12 : 0.0,
              rationale: internalMismatch
                  ? 'Observed authentication document text conflicts with separately entered provider or certificate details. This is an internal documentation inconsistency, not a provider-verification result.'
                  : providerVerified
                  ? 'An external authentication adapter reported a verified provider result.'
                  : 'Autograph and COA evidence was recorded without external provider verification.',
            ),
          );
          break;
      }
    }
  }

  static void _addLegacyCheck({
    required bool? value,
    required String id,
    required AtlasInspectionArea area,
    required AtlasEvidenceCategory category,
    required String goodTitle,
    required String warningTitle,
    required String warningMessage,
    required List<AtlasAuthenticitySignal> displaySignals,
    required List<AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal> evaluatedSignals,
    required double confidence,
  }) {
    if (value == null) return;

    if (value) {
      displaySignals.add(
        AtlasAuthenticitySignal(
          id: id,
          title: goodTitle,
          message: 'Collector-confirmed visual observation.',
          level: AtlasAuthenticitySignalLevel.support,
          weight: 5,
        ),
      );
      return;
    }

    displaySignals.add(
      AtlasAuthenticitySignal(
        id: id,
        title: warningTitle,
        message: warningMessage,
        level: AtlasAuthenticitySignalLevel.caution,
        weight: -8,
      ),
    );
    evaluatedSignals.add(
      AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal(
        id: id,
        area: area,
        category: category,
        severity: AtlasSignalSeverity.advisory,
        disposition: AtlasSignalDisposition.discrepancy,
        confidence: confidence,
        baseWeight: 0.12,
        rationale: warningMessage,
      ),
    );
  }

  static _ConcernAggregation _aggregateConcern(
    List<AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal> signals,
    List<String> audit,
  ) {
    final Map<AtlasEvidenceCategory, List<AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal>>
    grouped = <AtlasEvidenceCategory, List<AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal>>{};

    for (final AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal signal in signals) {
      if (!signal.isDiscrepancy) continue;
      grouped
          .putIfAbsent(
            signal.category,
            () => <AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal>[],
          )
          .add(signal);
    }

    double score = 0.0;
    int criticalCategories = 0;

    for (final MapEntry<
          AtlasEvidenceCategory,
          List<AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal>
        >
        entry
        in grouped.entries) {
      double raw = 0.0;
      bool hasCritical = false;
      for (final AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal signal in entry.value) {
        raw += signal.weightedConcern;
        hasCritical =
            hasCritical || signal.severity == AtlasSignalSeverity.critical;
      }

      if (hasCritical) {
        criticalCategories++;
      }

      final double clamped = raw.clamp(0.0, categoryConcernCap).toDouble();
      if (raw > categoryConcernCap) {
        audit.add(
          '${entry.key.name} concern was clamped from ${raw.toStringAsFixed(3)} to ${categoryConcernCap.toStringAsFixed(3)} to prevent correlated evidence from compounding.',
        );
      }
      score += clamped;
    }

    return _ConcernAggregation(
      score: score.clamp(0.0, 1.0).toDouble(),
      criticalCategories: criticalCategories,
    );
  }

  static AtlasInspectionReferenceCriteria _fallbackReference(
    ScanSession session,
  ) {
    final resolved = session.resolvedProduct;
    if (resolved == null) {
      return AtlasInspectionReferenceCriteria.unknown(
        productId: 'scan-${session.sessionId}',
      );
    }

    final List<String> variants = <String>[];
    if (resolved.variantName != null &&
        resolved.variantName!.trim().isNotEmpty) {
      variants.add(resolved.variantName!.trim());
    }

    return AtlasInspectionReferenceCriteria(
      productId: resolved.productId.value,
      upc: resolved.primaryBarcode,
      coverageLevel: AtlasReferenceCoverageLevel.limited,
      completenessScore: 0.35,
      legitimateVariantTypes: variants,
      provenance: <AtlasReferenceProvenance>[
        AtlasReferenceProvenance(
          sourceType: AtlasReferenceSourceType.inferredEstimate,
          sourceIdentifier: 'resolved-product-snapshot',
          lastVerifiedAt: DateTime.now().toUtc(),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  static AtlasAuthenticityAssessment _assessment({
    required AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel concernLevel,
    required double evidenceConfidence,
    required double referenceCoverage,
    required double inspectionCoverage,
    required double aggregatedConcernScore,
    required String summary,
    required List<AtlasAuthenticitySignal> displaySignals,
    required List<AtlasEvaluatedAuthenticitySignal> evaluatedSignals,
    required List<AtlasInspectionArea> uninspectedAreas,
    required List<String> audit,
    required AtlasProductionStamp? productionStamp,
  }) {
    final AtlasAuthenticityStatus status = switch (concernLevel) {
      AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.insufficientData =>
        AtlasAuthenticityStatus.insufficientEvidence,
      AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.reviewRecommended ||
      AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.significantInconsistencies ||
      AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.strongCounterfeitIndicators =>
        AtlasAuthenticityStatus.reviewRecommended,
      AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.noObviousConcerns =>
        inspectionCoverage >= 0.70
            ? AtlasAuthenticityStatus.strongEvidence
            : AtlasAuthenticityStatus.developingEvidence,
    };

    return AtlasAuthenticityAssessment(
      status: status,
      concernLevel: concernLevel,
      evidenceConfidence: evidenceConfidence,
      referenceCoverage: referenceCoverage,
      inspectionCoverage: inspectionCoverage,
      aggregatedConcernScore: aggregatedConcernScore,
      summary: summary,
      signals: displaySignals,
      evaluatedSignals: evaluatedSignals,
      uninspectedAreas: uninspectedAreas,
      auditLog: audit,
      productionStamp: productionStamp,
    );
  }

  static String _summaryForConcern(AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel concernLevel) {
    return switch (concernLevel) {
      AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.noObviousConcerns =>
        'No obvious concerns were found in the inspected evidence. This is not a guarantee of authenticity.',
      AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.reviewRecommended =>
        'One or more low-to-moderate inconsistencies should be reviewed before relying on this inspection.',
      AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.significantInconsistencies =>
        'Significant inconsistencies were found across the inspected evidence. Collector review is recommended.',
      AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.strongCounterfeitIndicators =>
        'Multiple independent high-signal contradictions were found. This is a warning assessment, not a definitive counterfeit determination.',
      AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.insufficientData =>
        'There is not enough reliable evidence or reference coverage to form an authenticity assessment.',
    };
  }
}

final class _ConcernAggregation {
  const _ConcernAggregation({
    required this.score,
    required this.criticalCategories,
  });

  final double score;
  final int criticalCategories;
}

FILE: .\lib\atlas\scan\authenticity\atlas_visual_inspection_service.dart
============================================================
import '../contributions/contribution_conflict_policy.dart';
import '../contributions/visual_inspection_contribution.dart';
import '../engine/engine_context.dart';
import '../models/scan_session.dart';
import '../variant/atlas_variant_resolution_bridge.dart';
import 'atlas_authenticity_engine.dart';
import 'engines/atlas_high_signal_inspection_pipeline.dart';
import 'models/atlas_visual_inspection.dart';

class AtlasVisualInspectionService {
  const AtlasVisualInspectionService({
    this.authenticityEngine = const AtlasAuthenticityEngine(),
    this.highSignalPipeline = const AtlasHighSignalInspectionPipeline(),
    this.variantResolutionBridge = const AtlasVariantResolutionBridge(),
  });

  final AtlasAuthenticityEngine authenticityEngine;
  final AtlasHighSignalInspectionPipeline highSignalPipeline;
  final AtlasVariantResolutionBridge variantResolutionBridge;

  Future<ScanSession> inspect({
    required ScanSession session,
    required AtlasVisualInspection inspection,
  }) async {
    final AtlasVisualInspection enrichedInspection = highSignalPipeline.enrich(
      sessionId: session.sessionId,
      inspection: inspection,
      expectedResolvedVariant: session.resolvedProduct?.variantName,
    );

    var updated = session;

    for (final image in enrichedInspection.images) {
      updated = updated.addImage(image);
    }

    updated = updated.addContribution(
      VisualInspectionContribution(
        contributionId: 'visual-inspection-${session.sessionId}',
        sourceEngineId: 'atlas.visual-inspection',
        createdAt: DateTime.now().toUtc(),
        inspection: enrichedInspection,
        confidence: enrichedInspection.resolvedEvidenceConfidence,
        attributes: <String, Object?>{
          'inspectionAreas': enrichedInspection.inspectedAreas
              .map((area) => area.name)
              .toList(growable: false),
          'structuredObservationCount': enrichedInspection.observations.length,
          'hasReferenceCriteria': enrichedInspection.referenceCriteria != null,
          'highSignalEnginesApplied': true,
        },
      ),
      conflictPolicy: ContributionConflictPolicy.replaceExisting,
    );

    // Scanner 5D interprets the structured sticker evidence for the existing
    // variant-resolution model. This intentionally happens outside the session
    // projector so visual evidence cannot replace canonical product identity.
    final variantResolution = variantResolutionBridge.resolve(updated);
    if (variantResolution != null) {
      updated = updated.copyWith(variantResolution: variantResolution);
    }

    final result = await authenticityEngine.process(
      updated,
      EngineContext(
        requestId: session.sessionId,
        createdAt: DateTime.now().toUtc(),
        deviceType: EngineDeviceType.mobile,
      ),
    );

    return result.session;
  }
}

import '../../catalog/models/atlas_catalog_product.dart';
import '../contributions/contribution_collection.dart';
import '../contributions/contribution_conflict_policy.dart';
import '../contributions/intelligence_contribution.dart';
import '../contributions/authenticity_contribution.dart';
import '../contributions/visual_inspection_contribution.dart';
import '../../identity/product/atlas_product_id.dart';
import '../projection/scan_session_projector.dart';
import '../resolution/resolved_product_snapshot.dart';
import '../results/collector_item_status.dart';
import '../results/market_value_snapshot.dart';
import '../results/scan_variant_status.dart';
import '../workflow/identity_resolution_status.dart';
import '../workflow/scan_intent.dart';
import '../workflow/scan_performance_mode.dart';
import 'scan_code.dart';
import 'scan_image.dart';
import 'scanner_type.dart';
import 'visual_identity.dart';

// Describes the lifecycle of an Atlas scan session.
enum ScanSessionStatus {
  created,
  scanning,
  analyzing,
  resolving,
  awaitingConfirmation,
  completed,
  cancelled,
  failed,
}

// Immutable orchestration model for one Atlas scanning workflow.
//
// A session begins when a collector opens the scanner and continues
// through code detection, visual analysis, identity resolution,
// market lookup, Vault matching, and briefing generation.
class ScanSession {
  ScanSession({
    required this.sessionId,
    required this.startedAt,
    required this.intent,
    required this.performanceMode,
    required this.scannerType,
    this.status = ScanSessionStatus.created,
    this.completedAt,
    List<ScanCode> detectedCodes = const [],
    this.bestCandidate,
    this.visualIdentity = const VisualIdentity(),
    this.resolutionStatus = IdentityResolutionStatus.notStarted,
    this.atlasProductId,
    this.resolvedProduct,
    this.marketSnapshot,
    this.collectorStatus,
    this.variantResolution,
    ContributionCollection? contributions,
    List<ScanImage> images = const [],
    this.confidence = 0.0,
    this.isOffline = false,
    this.requiresSync = false,
    List<String> notes = const [],
    List<String> warnings = const [],
  }) : detectedCodes = List<ScanCode>.unmodifiable(detectedCodes),
       contributions = contributions ?? ContributionCollection.empty,
       images = List<ScanImage>.unmodifiable(images),
       notes = List<String>.unmodifiable(notes),
       warnings = List<String>.unmodifiable(warnings);

  final String sessionId;

  final DateTime startedAt;

  final DateTime? completedAt;

  final ScanSessionStatus status;

  final ScanIntent intent;

  final ScanPerformanceMode performanceMode;

  final ScannerType scannerType;

  final List<ScanCode> detectedCodes;

  final ScanCode? bestCandidate;

  final VisualIdentity visualIdentity;

  final IdentityResolutionStatus resolutionStatus;

  final AtlasProductId? atlasProductId;

  final ResolvedProductSnapshot? resolvedProduct;

  final MarketValueSnapshot? marketSnapshot;

  final CollectorItemStatus? collectorStatus;

  final ScanVariantResolution? variantResolution;

  final ContributionCollection contributions;

  final List<ScanImage> images;

  final double confidence;

  final bool isOffline;

  final bool requiresSync;

  final List<String> notes;

  final List<String> warnings;

  bool get hasDetectedCodes {
    return detectedCodes.isNotEmpty;
  }

  bool get hasBestCandidate {
    return bestCandidate != null;
  }

  bool get hasVisualEvidence {
    return visualIdentity.isNotEmpty;
  }

  bool get hasResolvedProduct {
    return atlasProductId != null && resolutionStatus.isResolved;
  }

  bool get hasMarketData {
    return marketSnapshot?.hasMarketEvidence ?? false;
  }

  bool get hasCollectorStatus {
    return collectorStatus != null;
  }

  bool get hasVariantResolution {
    return variantResolution != null;
  }

  bool get hasContributions {
    return contributions.isNotEmpty;
  }

  VisualInspectionContribution? get visualInspectionContribution =>
      latestContributionOfType<VisualInspectionContribution>();

  AuthenticityContribution? get authenticityContribution =>
      latestContributionOfType<AuthenticityContribution>();

  bool get hasAuthenticityAssessment => authenticityContribution != null;

  int get contributionCount {
    return contributions.length;
  }

  bool hasContributionOfType<T extends IntelligenceContribution>() {
    return contributions.ofType<T>().isNotEmpty;
  }

  T? latestContributionOfType<T extends IntelligenceContribution>() {
    return contributions.latestOfType<T>();
  }

  bool get requiresVariantConfirmation {
    return variantResolution?.requiresConfirmation ?? false;
  }

  bool get hasImages {
    return images.isNotEmpty;
  }

  bool get hasOriginalImage {
    return images.any((ScanImage image) => image.isOriginal);
  }

  bool get hasProcessedImage {
    return images.any((ScanImage image) => image.isProcessed);
  }

  bool get isActive {
    return status == ScanSessionStatus.scanning ||
        status == ScanSessionStatus.analyzing ||
        status == ScanSessionStatus.resolving;
  }

  bool get isCompleted {
    return status == ScanSessionStatus.completed;
  }

  AtlasCatalogProduct? get catalogProduct {
    return resolvedProduct?.toCatalogProduct();
  }

  bool get requiresUserAction {
    return status == ScanSessionStatus.awaitingConfirmation ||
        resolutionStatus.requiresUserReview ||
        requiresVariantConfirmation;
  }

  bool get canBuildBriefing {
    return hasResolvedProduct && resolutionStatus.isResolved;
  }

  Duration get elapsed {
    final DateTime end = completedAt ?? DateTime.now();

    return end.difference(startedAt);
  }

  ScanImage? get originalImage {
    for (final ScanImage image in images) {
      if (image.type == ScanImageType.original) {
        return image;
      }
    }

    return null;
  }

  ScanImage? get processedImage {
    for (final ScanImage image in images) {
      if (image.type == ScanImageType.processed) {
        return image;
      }
    }

    return null;
  }

  ScanSession copyWith({
    String? sessionId,
    DateTime? startedAt,
    DateTime? completedAt,
    ScanSessionStatus? status,
    ScanIntent? intent,
    ScanPerformanceMode? performanceMode,
    ScannerType? scannerType,
    List<ScanCode>? detectedCodes,
    ScanCode? bestCandidate,
    VisualIdentity? visualIdentity,
    IdentityResolutionStatus? resolutionStatus,
    AtlasProductId? atlasProductId,
    ResolvedProductSnapshot? resolvedProduct,
    MarketValueSnapshot? marketSnapshot,
    CollectorItemStatus? collectorStatus,
    ScanVariantResolution? variantResolution,
    ContributionCollection? contributions,
    List<ScanImage>? images,
    double? confidence,
    bool? isOffline,
    bool? requiresSync,
    List<String>? notes,
    List<String>? warnings,
  }) {
    return ScanSession(
      sessionId: sessionId ?? this.sessionId,
      startedAt: startedAt ?? this.startedAt,
      completedAt: completedAt ?? this.completedAt,
      status: status ?? this.status,
      intent: intent ?? this.intent,
      performanceMode: performanceMode ?? this.performanceMode,
      scannerType: scannerType ?? this.scannerType,
      detectedCodes: detectedCodes ?? this.detectedCodes,
      bestCandidate: bestCandidate ?? this.bestCandidate,
      visualIdentity: visualIdentity ?? this.visualIdentity,
      resolutionStatus: resolutionStatus ?? this.resolutionStatus,
      atlasProductId: atlasProductId ?? this.atlasProductId,
      resolvedProduct: resolvedProduct ?? this.resolvedProduct,
      marketSnapshot: marketSnapshot ?? this.marketSnapshot,
      collectorStatus: collectorStatus ?? this.collectorStatus,
      variantResolution: variantResolution ?? this.variantResolution,
      contributions: contributions ?? this.contributions,
      images: images ?? this.images,
      confidence: confidence ?? this.confidence,
      isOffline: isOffline ?? this.isOffline,
      requiresSync: requiresSync ?? this.requiresSync,
      notes: notes ?? this.notes,
      warnings: warnings ?? this.warnings,
    );
  }

  ScanSession addDetectedCode(ScanCode code, {bool makeBestCandidate = false}) {
    final bool alreadyDetected = detectedCodes.any((ScanCode existing) {
      return existing.format == code.format &&
          existing.normalizedValue == code.normalizedValue;
    });

    final List<ScanCode> updatedCodes = alreadyDetected
        ? detectedCodes
        : <ScanCode>[...detectedCodes, code];

    return copyWith(
      detectedCodes: updatedCodes,
      bestCandidate: makeBestCandidate ? code : bestCandidate,
    );
  }

  ScanSession addContribution(
    IntelligenceContribution contribution, {
    ContributionConflictPolicy conflictPolicy =
        ContributionConflictPolicy.reject,
  }) {
    final ContributionCollection updatedContributions = contributions.add(
      contribution,
      conflictPolicy: conflictPolicy,
    );

    return _withProjectedContributions(updatedContributions);
  }

  ScanSession addContributions(
    Iterable<IntelligenceContribution> values, {
    ContributionConflictPolicy conflictPolicy =
        ContributionConflictPolicy.reject,
  }) {
    final ContributionCollection updatedContributions = contributions.addAll(
      values,
      conflictPolicy: conflictPolicy,
    );

    return _withProjectedContributions(updatedContributions);
  }

  ScanSession _withProjectedContributions(
    ContributionCollection updatedContributions,
  ) {
    final ScanSessionProjection projection = ScanSessionProjector.project(
      contributions: updatedContributions,
      currentStatus: status,
      currentResolutionStatus: resolutionStatus,
      currentAtlasProductId: atlasProductId,
      currentResolvedProduct: resolvedProduct,
      currentConfidence: confidence,
      isOffline: isOffline,
    );

    return copyWith(
      contributions: updatedContributions,
      status: projection.status,
      resolutionStatus: projection.resolutionStatus,
      atlasProductId: projection.atlasProductId,
      resolvedProduct: projection.resolvedProduct,
      confidence: projection.confidence,
    );
  }

  ScanSession removeContributionById(String contributionId) {
    final ContributionCollection updated = contributions.removeById(
      contributionId,
    );

    if (identical(updated, contributions)) {
      return this;
    }

    return copyWith(contributions: updated);
  }

  ScanSession removeContributionsFromEngine(String engineId) {
    final ContributionCollection updated = contributions.removeFromEngine(
      engineId,
    );

    if (identical(updated, contributions)) {
      return this;
    }

    return copyWith(contributions: updated);
  }

  ScanSession addImage(ScanImage image) {
    final List<ScanImage> updatedImages =
        images
            .where((ScanImage existing) => existing.imageId != image.imageId)
            .toList()
          ..add(image);

    return copyWith(images: updatedImages);
  }

  ScanSession addNote(String note) {
    final String normalized = note.trim();

    if (normalized.isEmpty || notes.contains(normalized)) {
      return this;
    }

    return copyWith(notes: <String>[...notes, normalized]);
  }

  ScanSession addWarning(String warning) {
    final String normalized = warning.trim();

    if (normalized.isEmpty || warnings.contains(normalized)) {
      return this;
    }

    return copyWith(warnings: <String>[...warnings, normalized]);
  }

  @override
  String toString() {
    return 'ScanSession('
        'sessionId: $sessionId, '
        'status: ${status.name}, '
        'intent: ${intent.name}, '
        'codes: ${detectedCodes.length}, '
        'images: ${images.length}, '
        'resolutionStatus: ${resolutionStatus.name}, '
        'atlasProductId: $atlasProductId, '
        'confidence: $confidence, '
        'isOffline: $isOffline, '
        'requiresSync: $requiresSync'
        ')';
  }
}

// Describes whether Atlas has identified the exact physical variant.
enum ScanVariantStatus {
  exactMatch,
  likelyMatch,
  multipleCandidates,
  visualConfirmationRequired,
  unknown,
}

// Immutable summary of variant resolution.
class ScanVariantResolution {
  const ScanVariantResolution({
    this.status = ScanVariantStatus.unknown,
    this.candidateProductIds = const [],
    this.confirmedTraits = const [],
    this.unconfirmedTraits = const [],
    this.confidence = 0.0,
  });

  final ScanVariantStatus status;

  final List<String> candidateProductIds;

  final List<String> confirmedTraits;

  final List<String> unconfirmedTraits;

  final double confidence;

  bool get isExact {
    return status == ScanVariantStatus.exactMatch;
  }

  bool get requiresConfirmation {
    return status ==
        ScanVariantStatus.multipleCandidates ||
        status ==
            ScanVariantStatus.visualConfirmationRequired;
  }

  bool get hasMultipleCandidates {
    return candidateProductIds.length > 1;
  }

  ScanVariantResolution copyWith({
    ScanVariantStatus? status,
    List<String>? candidateProductIds,
    List<String>? confirmedTraits,
    List<String>? unconfirmedTraits,
    double? confidence,
  }) {
    return ScanVariantResolution(
      status: status ?? this.status,
      candidateProductIds:
      candidateProductIds ??
          this.candidateProductIds,
      confirmedTraits:
      confirmedTraits ?? this.confirmedTraits,
      unconfirmedTraits:
      unconfirmedTraits ??
          this.unconfirmedTraits,
      confidence: confidence ?? this.confidence,
    );
  }
}

// Describes the direction of a collectible's recent market movement.
enum MarketTrendDirection {
  rising,
  falling,
  stable,
  unknown,
}

// Identifies the confidence Atlas has in a market value estimate.
enum MarketValueConfidence {
  low,
  medium,
  high,
  verified,
  unknown,
}

// Immutable pricing snapshot returned for a collectible.
//
// This represents a value at a specific point in time. It should not
// be stored as permanent product identity data because prices change.
class MarketValueSnapshot {
  const MarketValueSnapshot({
    required this.currencyCode,
    required this.lastUpdated,
    this.estimatedValue,
    this.retailPrice,
    this.recentSaleLow,
    this.recentSaleHigh,
    this.lastSalePrice,
    this.trendPercentage,
    this.trendDirection = MarketTrendDirection.unknown,
    this.confidence = MarketValueConfidence.unknown,
    this.recentSalesCount = 0,
    this.sourceNames = const [],
  });

  final String currencyCode;

  final double? estimatedValue;

  final double? retailPrice;

  final double? recentSaleLow;

  final double? recentSaleHigh;

  final double? lastSalePrice;

  final double? trendPercentage;

  final MarketTrendDirection trendDirection;

  final MarketValueConfidence confidence;

  final int recentSalesCount;

  final DateTime lastUpdated;

  final List<String> sourceNames;

  bool get hasEstimatedValue => estimatedValue != null;

  bool get hasRetailPrice => retailPrice != null;

  bool get hasRecentRange {
    return recentSaleLow != null && recentSaleHigh != null;
  }

  bool get hasLastSale => lastSalePrice != null;

  bool get hasTrend => trendPercentage != null;

  bool get hasMarketEvidence {
    return hasEstimatedValue ||
        hasRecentRange ||
        hasLastSale ||
        recentSalesCount > 0;
  }

  bool get isStale {
    return DateTime.now().difference(lastUpdated).inDays > 30;
  }

  MarketValueSnapshot copyWith({
    String? currencyCode,
    double? estimatedValue,
    double? retailPrice,
    double? recentSaleLow,
    double? recentSaleHigh,
    double? lastSalePrice,
    double? trendPercentage,
    MarketTrendDirection? trendDirection,
    MarketValueConfidence? confidence,
    int? recentSalesCount,
    DateTime? lastUpdated,
    List<String>? sourceNames,
  }) {
    return MarketValueSnapshot(
      currencyCode: currencyCode ?? this.currencyCode,
      estimatedValue:
      estimatedValue ?? this.estimatedValue,
      retailPrice: retailPrice ?? this.retailPrice,
      recentSaleLow:
      recentSaleLow ?? this.recentSaleLow,
      recentSaleHigh:
      recentSaleHigh ?? this.recentSaleHigh,
      lastSalePrice:
      lastSalePrice ?? this.lastSalePrice,
      trendPercentage:
      trendPercentage ?? this.trendPercentage,
      trendDirection:
      trendDirection ?? this.trendDirection,
      confidence: confidence ?? this.confidence,
      recentSalesCount:
      recentSalesCount ?? this.recentSalesCount,
      lastUpdated: lastUpdated ?? this.lastUpdated,
      sourceNames: sourceNames ?? this.sourceNames,
    );
  }
}

// Describes the scanned product's relationship to the user's Vault.
enum CollectorItemRelationship {
  notInVault,
  owned,
  wishlisted,
  ownedAndWishlisted,
  unknown,
}

// Immutable user-specific status for a scanned collectible.
//
// This data belongs to the collector and must remain separate from the
// shared catalog product.
class CollectorItemStatus {
  const CollectorItemStatus({
    this.relationship =
        CollectorItemRelationship.unknown,
    this.ownedQuantity = 0,
    this.collectionNames = const [],
    this.possibleDuplicateCount = 0,
  });

  final CollectorItemRelationship relationship;

  final int ownedQuantity;

  final List<String> collectionNames;

  final int possibleDuplicateCount;

  bool get isOwned {
    return relationship ==
        CollectorItemRelationship.owned ||
        relationship ==
            CollectorItemRelationship.ownedAndWishlisted;
  }

  bool get isWishlisted {
    return relationship ==
        CollectorItemRelationship.wishlisted ||
        relationship ==
            CollectorItemRelationship.ownedAndWishlisted;
  }

  bool get isNotInVault {
    return relationship ==
        CollectorItemRelationship.notInVault;
  }

  bool get hasPossibleDuplicates {
    return possibleDuplicateCount > 0;
  }

  CollectorItemStatus copyWith({
    CollectorItemRelationship? relationship,
    int? ownedQuantity,
    List<String>? collectionNames,
    int? possibleDuplicateCount,
  }) {
    return CollectorItemStatus(
      relationship:
      relationship ?? this.relationship,
      ownedQuantity:
      ownedQuantity ?? this.ownedQuantity,
      collectionNames:
      collectionNames ?? this.collectionNames,
      possibleDuplicateCount:
      possibleDuplicateCount ??
          this.possibleDuplicateCount,
    );
  }
}

import '../models/scan_code.dart';
import 'atlas_scan_ledger_entry.dart';

/// In-memory ledger for one continuous scanner workflow.
///
/// Entries are keyed by physical track ID rather than barcode or product ID so
/// two identical collectibles with the same UPC can remain separate items.
class AtlasScanLedger {
  final Map<String, AtlasScanLedgerEntry> _entries =
      <String, AtlasScanLedgerEntry>{};

  List<AtlasScanLedgerEntry> get entries {
    return List<AtlasScanLedgerEntry>.unmodifiable(_entries.values);
  }

  AtlasScanLedgerEntry? entryForTrack(String trackId) {
    return _entries[trackId.trim()];
  }

  bool containsTrack(String trackId) {
    return _entries.containsKey(trackId.trim());
  }

  AtlasScanLedgerEntry observe({
    required String trackId,
    required ScanCode stableCode,
    required DateTime at,
  }) {
    final String normalizedTrackId = _normalizeTrackId(trackId);
    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry? existing = _entries[normalizedTrackId];

    if (existing != null) {
      return existing;
    }

    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry entry = AtlasScanLedgerEntry(
      trackId: normalizedTrackId,
      stableCode: stableCode,
      status: AtlasScanLedgerStatus.observed,
      createdAt: at,
      updatedAt: at,
      attemptCount: 0,
    );

    _entries[normalizedTrackId] = entry;

    return entry;
  }

  AtlasScanLedgerEntry markQueued({
    required String trackId,
    required DateTime at,
    bool allowRetry = false,
  }) {
    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry entry = _requireEntry(trackId);

    if (entry.isCommitted) {
      throw StateError(
        'Committed track "${entry.trackId}" cannot be queued again.',
      );
    }

    if (entry.hasFailed && !allowRetry) {
      throw StateError(
        'Failed track "${entry.trackId}" requires an explicit retry.',
      );
    }

    if (entry.isQueued || entry.isResolving) {
      return entry;
    }

    if (entry.isResolved || entry.needsReview) {
      throw StateError(
        'Resolved track "${entry.trackId}" cannot be queued again.',
      );
    }

    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry updated = entry.copyWith(
      status: AtlasScanLedgerStatus.queued,
      updatedAt: at,
      clearFailureMessage: allowRetry,
    );

    _entries[entry.trackId] = updated;

    return updated;
  }

  AtlasScanLedgerEntry markResolving({
    required String trackId,
    required DateTime at,
  }) {
    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry entry = _requireEntry(trackId);

    if (!entry.isQueued) {
      throw StateError(
        'Track "${entry.trackId}" must be queued before resolving.',
      );
    }

    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry updated = entry.copyWith(
      status: AtlasScanLedgerStatus.resolving,
      updatedAt: at,
      attemptCount: entry.attemptCount + 1,
      clearFailureMessage: true,
    );

    _entries[entry.trackId] = updated;

    return updated;
  }

  AtlasScanLedgerEntry markResolved({
    required String trackId,
    required String resolutionSessionId,
    required DateTime at,
  }) {
    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry entry = _requireEntry(trackId);

    if (!entry.isResolving) {
      throw StateError(
        'Track "${entry.trackId}" must be resolving before it can resolve.',
      );
    }

    final String normalizedSessionId = resolutionSessionId.trim();

    if (normalizedSessionId.isEmpty) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(
        resolutionSessionId,
        'resolutionSessionId',
        'resolutionSessionId cannot be empty.',
      );
    }

    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry updated = entry.copyWith(
      status: AtlasScanLedgerStatus.resolved,
      updatedAt: at,
      resolutionSessionId: normalizedSessionId,
      clearFailureMessage: true,
    );

    _entries[entry.trackId] = updated;

    return updated;
  }

  AtlasScanLedgerEntry markNeedsReview({
    required String trackId,
    required String resolutionSessionId,
    required DateTime at,
  }) {
    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry entry = _requireEntry(trackId);

    if (!entry.isResolving) {
      throw StateError(
        'Track "${entry.trackId}" must be resolving before review.',
      );
    }

    final String normalizedSessionId = resolutionSessionId.trim();

    if (normalizedSessionId.isEmpty) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(
        resolutionSessionId,
        'resolutionSessionId',
        'resolutionSessionId cannot be empty.',
      );
    }

    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry updated = entry.copyWith(
      status: AtlasScanLedgerStatus.needsReview,
      updatedAt: at,
      resolutionSessionId: normalizedSessionId,
      clearFailureMessage: true,
    );

    _entries[entry.trackId] = updated;

    return updated;
  }

  AtlasScanLedgerEntry markFailed({
    required String trackId,
    required Object error,
    required DateTime at,
  }) {
    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry entry = _requireEntry(trackId);

    if (!entry.isResolving) {
      throw StateError(
        'Track "${entry.trackId}" must be resolving before it can fail.',
      );
    }

    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry updated = entry.copyWith(
      status: AtlasScanLedgerStatus.failed,
      updatedAt: at,
      failureMessage: error.toString(),
    );

    _entries[entry.trackId] = updated;

    return updated;
  }

  AtlasScanLedgerEntry markCommitted({
    required String trackId,
    required DateTime at,
  }) {
    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry entry = _requireEntry(trackId);

    if (entry.isCommitted) {
      return entry;
    }

    if (!entry.isResolved && !entry.needsReview) {
      throw StateError(
        'Track "${entry.trackId}" must be resolved before commit.',
      );
    }

    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry updated = entry.copyWith(
      status: AtlasScanLedgerStatus.committed,
      updatedAt: at,
      committedAt: at,
    );

    _entries[entry.trackId] = updated;

    return updated;
  }

  AtlasScanLedgerEntry applyInspectionReview({
    required String trackId,
    required bool requiresReview,
    required DateTime at,
  }) {
    final entry = _requireEntry(trackId);
    if (entry.isCommitted || (!entry.isResolved && !entry.needsReview)) {
      throw StateError(
        'Track "${entry.trackId}" is not available for inspection review.',
      );
    }
    final updated = entry.copyWith(
      status: requiresReview
          ? AtlasScanLedgerStatus.needsReview
          : AtlasScanLedgerStatus.resolved,
      updatedAt: at,
    );
    _entries[entry.trackId] = updated;
    return updated;
  }

  void clear() {
    _entries.clear();
  }

  AtlasScanLedgerEntry _requireEntry(String trackId) {
    final String normalizedTrackId = _normalizeTrackId(trackId);
    final AtlasScanLedgerEntry? entry = _entries[normalizedTrackId];

    if (entry == null) {
      throw StateError(
        'No scan ledger entry exists for track "$normalizedTrackId".',
      );
    }

    return entry;
  }

  String _normalizeTrackId(String trackId) {
    final String normalized = trackId.trim();

    if (normalized.isEmpty) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(trackId, 'trackId', 'trackId cannot be empty.');
    }

    return normalized;
  }
}

import '../../scan/models/scan_session.dart';
import '../../scan/workflow/identity_resolution_status.dart';
import '../models/atlas_scan_intelligence.dart';
import 'atlas_engine.dart';

class AtlasScanIntelligenceEngine
    extends AtlasEngine<ScanSession, AtlasScanIntelligence> {
  const AtlasScanIntelligenceEngine();

  @override
  String get id => 'atlas.scan-intelligence';

  @override
  String get name => 'Atlas Scan Intelligence Engine';

  @override
  String get description {
    return 'Produces prioritized collector-facing intelligence from a '
        'projected Atlas scan session.';
  }

  @override
  String get version => '1.1.0';

  @override
  List<String> validateInput(
      ScanSession input,
      ) {
    final List<String> issues = <String>[];

    if (input.sessionId.trim().isEmpty) {
      issues.add(
        'The scan session must have a session ID.',
      );
    }

    if (!input.hasDetectedCodes) {
      issues.add(
        'The scan session does not contain detected code evidence.',
      );
    }

    return List<String>.unmodifiable(
      issues,
    );

  }
  List<AtlasScanInsight> _deduplicateInsights(
      List<AtlasScanInsight> insights,
      ) {
    final Map<String, AtlasScanInsight> byId =
    <String, AtlasScanInsight>{};

    for (final AtlasScanInsight insight in insights) {
      final AtlasScanInsight? existing =
      byId[insight.id];

      if (existing == null ||
          insight.effectivePriorityWeight <
              existing.effectivePriorityWeight) {
        byId[insight.id] = insight;
      }
    }

    return byId.values.toList(
      growable: false,
    );
  }

  @override
  AtlasScanIntelligence process(
      ScanSession input,
      ) {
    final List<AtlasScanInsight> insights =
    <AtlasScanInsight>[];

    _addIdentityInsight(
      session: input,
      insights: insights,
    );

    _addConfidenceInsight(
      session: input,
      insights: insights,
    );

    _addVariantInsight(
      session: input,
      insights: insights,
    );

    _addCollectorInsight(
      session: input,
      insights: insights,
    );

    _addMarketInsight(
      session: input,
      insights: insights,
    );

    _addSyncInsight(
      session: input,
      insights: insights,
    );

    _addWarningInsights(
      session: input,
      insights: insights,
    );

    final List<AtlasScanInsight> finalInsights =
    _deduplicateInsights(
      insights,
    );

    finalInsights.sort(
          (
          AtlasScanInsight first,
          AtlasScanInsight second,
          ) {
        final int priorityComparison =
        first.effectivePriorityWeight.compareTo(
          second.effectivePriorityWeight,
        );

        if (priorityComparison != 0) {
          return priorityComparison;
        }

        return first.id.compareTo(
          second.id,
        );
      },
    );

    return AtlasScanIntelligence(
      sessionId: input.sessionId,
      generatedAt: DateTime.now(),
      insights: finalInsights,
    );
  }

  void _addIdentityInsight({
    required ScanSession session,
    required List<AtlasScanInsight> insights,
  }) {
    switch (session.resolutionStatus) {
      case IdentityResolutionStatus.resolvedLocal:
        insights.add(
          AtlasScanInsight(
            id: 'identity-resolved-local',
            type: AtlasScanInsightType.identity,
            emoji: '✅',
            title: 'Product Identified',
            message:
            'Atlas identified this collectible using local intelligence.',
            priority: AtlasScanInsightPriority.normal,
            priorityWeight: 30,
            source: AtlasScanInsightSource.identity,
            requiresOnline: false,
            details: _identityDetails(
              session,
            ),
          ),
        );

      case IdentityResolutionStatus.resolvedRemote:
        insights.add(
          AtlasScanInsight(
            id: 'identity-resolved-remote',
            type: AtlasScanInsightType.identity,
            emoji: '🌐',
            title: 'Product Identified',
            message:
            'Atlas identified this collectible using connected catalog intelligence.',
            priority: AtlasScanInsightPriority.normal,
            priorityWeight: 30,
            source: AtlasScanInsightSource.identity,
            requiresOnline: true,
            details: _identityDetails(
              session,
            ),
          ),
        );

      case IdentityResolutionStatus.pendingSync:
        insights.add(
          AtlasScanInsight(
            id: 'identity-sync-pending',
            type: AtlasScanInsightType.identity,
            emoji: '✅',
            title: 'Product Identified',
            message:
            'Atlas identified this collectible locally. Additional connected intelligence is pending.',
            priority: AtlasScanInsightPriority.normal,
            priorityWeight: 30,
            source: AtlasScanInsightSource.identity,
            requiresOnline: false,
            details: _identityDetails(
              session,
            ),
          ),
        );

      case IdentityResolutionStatus.ambiguous:
        insights.add(
          const AtlasScanInsight(
            id: 'identity-ambiguous',
            type: AtlasScanInsightType.warning,
            emoji: '⚠️',
            title: 'Identity Review Required',
            message:
            'Atlas found more than one possible product identity.',
            priority: AtlasScanInsightPriority.high,
            priorityWeight: 10,
            source: AtlasScanInsightSource.identity,
            requiresOnline: false,
            recommendation:
            'Review the product, barcode, sticker, and variant details before continuing.',
          ),
        );

      case IdentityResolutionStatus.unresolved:
        insights.add(
          const AtlasScanInsight(
            id: 'identity-unresolved',
            type: AtlasScanInsightType.warning,
            emoji: '🔎',
            title: 'Product Not Identified',
            message:
            'Atlas could not determine a confirmed product identity from the available evidence.',
            priority: AtlasScanInsightPriority.high,
            priorityWeight: 10,
            source: AtlasScanInsightSource.identity,
            requiresOnline: false,
            recommendation:
            'Try scanning the barcode again or search the Atlas catalog manually.',
          ),
        );

      case IdentityResolutionStatus.failed:
        insights.add(
          const AtlasScanInsight(
            id: 'identity-failed',
            type: AtlasScanInsightType.warning,
            emoji: '🚨',
            title: 'Resolution Failed',
            message:
            'Atlas could not complete product identity resolution.',
            priority: AtlasScanInsightPriority.critical,
            priorityWeight: 5,
            source: AtlasScanInsightSource.system,
            requiresOnline: false,
            recommendation:
            'Review the scan evidence and attempt a new scan.',
          ),
        );

      case IdentityResolutionStatus.notStarted:
      case IdentityResolutionStatus.resolving:
        break;
    }
  }

  void _addConfidenceInsight({
    required ScanSession session,
    required List<AtlasScanInsight> insights,
  }) {
    final int? confidencePercent = _confidencePercent(
      session.confidence,
    );

    if (confidencePercent == null) {
      return;
    }

    final AtlasScanInsightPriority priority;
    final int priorityWeight;
    final String message;

    if (confidencePercent >= 90) {
      priority = AtlasScanInsightPriority.low;
      priorityWeight = 60;
      message =
      'Atlas has very high confidence in this product identification.';
    } else if (confidencePercent >= 75) {
      priority = AtlasScanInsightPriority.normal;
      priorityWeight = 55;
      message =
      'Atlas has strong confidence in this product identification.';
    } else if (confidencePercent >= 60) {
      priority = AtlasScanInsightPriority.normal;
      priorityWeight = 40;
      message =
      'Atlas has moderate confidence in this product identification.';
    } else {
      priority = AtlasScanInsightPriority.high;
      priorityWeight = 20;
      message =
      'Atlas has limited confidence in this product identification.';
    }

    insights.add(
      AtlasScanInsight(
        id: 'scan-confidence',
        type: AtlasScanInsightType.identity,
        emoji: '🎯',
        title: 'Identification Confidence',
        message: message,
        priority: priority,
        priorityWeight: priorityWeight,
        source: AtlasScanInsightSource.scan,
        requiresOnline: false,
        details: <String>[
          '$confidencePercent% confidence',
          '${session.contributionCount} intelligence '
              '${session.contributionCount == 1 ? 'contribution' : 'contributions'}',
        ],
        recommendation: confidencePercent < 60
            ? 'Confirm the product number, sticker, and variant before adding it to your Vault.'
            : null,
      ),
    );
  }

  void _addVariantInsight({
    required ScanSession session,
    required List<AtlasScanInsight> insights,
  }) {
    if (!session.hasVariantResolution) {
      return;
    }

    if (session.requiresVariantConfirmation) {
      insights.add(
        const AtlasScanInsight(
          id: 'variant-confirmation-required',
          type: AtlasScanInsightType.variant,
          emoji: '🧩',
          title: 'Variant Confirmation Required',
          message:
          'Atlas identified possible variant information that still needs collector confirmation.',
          priority: AtlasScanInsightPriority.high,
          priorityWeight: 15,
          source: AtlasScanInsightSource.identity,
          requiresOnline: false,
          recommendation:
          'Compare the sticker, finish, retailer exclusive, event, and packaging details.',
        ),
      );

      return;
    }

    insights.add(
      const AtlasScanInsight(
        id: 'variant-reviewed',
        type: AtlasScanInsightType.variant,
        emoji: '🏷️',
        title: 'Variant Evidence Reviewed',
        message:
        'Atlas processed the available variant evidence for this collectible.',
        priority: AtlasScanInsightPriority.low,
        priorityWeight: 80,
        source: AtlasScanInsightSource.identity,
        requiresOnline: false,
      ),
    );
  }

  void _addCollectorInsight({
    required ScanSession session,
    required List<AtlasScanInsight> insights,
  }) {
    if (!session.hasCollectorStatus) {
      return;
    }

    insights.add(
      const AtlasScanInsight(
        id: 'collector-status-available',
        type: AtlasScanInsightType.collection,
        emoji: '📦',
        title: 'Vault Comparison Available',
        message:
        'Atlas compared this collectible with the collector’s current Vault.',
        priority: AtlasScanInsightPriority.normal,
        priorityWeight: 50,
        source: AtlasScanInsightSource.collection,
        requiresOnline: false,
      ),
    );
  }

  void _addMarketInsight({
    required ScanSession session,
    required List<AtlasScanInsight> insights,
  }) {
    if (!session.hasMarketData) {
      return;
    }

    insights.add(
      const AtlasScanInsight(
        id: 'market-evidence-available',
        type: AtlasScanInsightType.market,
        emoji: '💰',
        title: 'Market Evidence Available',
        message:
        'Atlas found market evidence connected to this collectible.',
        priority: AtlasScanInsightPriority.normal,
        priorityWeight: 40,
        source: AtlasScanInsightSource.market,
        requiresOnline: true,
        recommendation:
        'Review the market snapshot before recording or changing the item value.',
      ),
    );
  }

  void _addSyncInsight({
    required ScanSession session,
    required List<AtlasScanInsight> insights,
  }) {
    if (!session.requiresSync) {
      return;
    }

    insights.add(
      AtlasScanInsight(
        id: 'sync-required',
        type: AtlasScanInsightType.recommendation,
        emoji: '☁️',
        title: 'Additional Intelligence Pending',
        message: session.isOffline
            ? 'Atlas is currently offline and cannot complete connected intelligence checks.'
            : 'Atlas has additional intelligence that still needs to synchronize.',
        priority: AtlasScanInsightPriority.normal,
        priorityWeight: 70,
        source: AtlasScanInsightSource.system,
        requiresOnline: true,
        recommendation:
        'Keep the scan session available until synchronization completes.',
      ),
    );
  }

  void _addWarningInsights({
    required ScanSession session,
    required List<AtlasScanInsight> insights,
  }) {
    for (
    int index = 0;
    index < session.warnings.length;
    index++
    ) {
      final String warning = session.warnings[index].trim();

      if (warning.isEmpty) {
        continue;
      }

      insights.add(
        AtlasScanInsight(
          id: 'session-warning-$index',
          type: AtlasScanInsightType.warning,
          emoji: '🚨',
          title: 'Scan Warning',
          message: warning,
          priority: AtlasScanInsightPriority.high,
          priorityWeight: 5,
          source: AtlasScanInsightSource.scan,
          requiresOnline: false,
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  List<String> _identityDetails(
      ScanSession session,
      ) {
    final List<String> details = <String>[];

    final String? productId =
    session.atlasProductId?.value.trim();

    if (productId != null && productId.isNotEmpty) {
      details.add(
        'Atlas Product ID: $productId',
      );
    }

    final String? productName =
    session.catalogProduct?.displayName.trim();

    if (productName != null && productName.isNotEmpty) {
      details.add(
        productName,
      );
    }

    return List<String>.unmodifiable(
      details,
    );
  }

  int? _confidencePercent(
      double confidence,
      ) {
    if (!confidence.isFinite || confidence <= 0) {
      return null;
    }

    final double normalized =
    confidence <= 1
        ? confidence * 100
        : confidence;

    return normalized.round().clamp(
      0,
      100,
    );
  }
}
