import '../../../models/pop.dart';
import '../../hero/models/atlas_hero_summary.dart';
import '../../identity/pop/pop_identity_rule.dart';
import '../models/atlas_collectibility_signal.dart';
import '../models/atlas_collectibility_summary.dart';

/// Builds a unified collectibility analysis for one collectible.
///
/// Version 1 deliberately reuses the trusted Hero score instead of
/// recreating or competing with the existing Hero scoring rules.
///
/// This engine adds:
/// - structured collectibility signals,
/// - confidence,
/// - collector-facing explanations,
/// - display recommendations,
/// - insurance-review recommendations,
/// - and market-readiness analysis.
class AtlasCollectibilityEngine {
  const AtlasCollectibilityEngine();

  AtlasCollectibilitySummary build({
    required Pop pop,
    required AtlasHeroSummary heroSummary,
    required List<PopIdentityBadgeData> identityBadges,
  }) {
    final signals = _buildSignals(
      pop: pop,
      identityBadges: identityBadges,
    );

    final strengths = _buildStrengths(
      heroSummary: heroSummary,
      signals: signals,
    );

    final opportunities = _buildOpportunities(pop);

    final score = heroSummary.normalizedScore;
    final tier = heroSummary.classification;

    final confidence = _buildConfidence(
      pop: pop,
      signalCount: signals.length,
    );

    final marketReadiness = _buildMarketReadiness(pop);

    return AtlasCollectibilitySummary(
      score: score,
      tier: tier,
      confidence: confidence,
      headline: _buildHeadline(
        pop: pop,
        tier: tier,
        signals: signals,
      ),
      explanation: _buildExplanation(
        tier: tier,
        confidence: confidence,
        signals: signals,
      ),
      strengths: List<String>.unmodifiable(strengths),
      opportunities: List<String>.unmodifiable(opportunities),
      signals: List<AtlasCollectibilitySignal>.unmodifiable(signals),
      isDisplayPiece: _isDisplayPiece(
        score: score,
        signals: signals,
      ),
      shouldReviewForInsurance: _shouldReviewForInsurance(
        pop: pop,
        score: score,
        signals: signals,
      ),
      marketReadiness: marketReadiness,
    );
  }

  List<AtlasCollectibilitySignal> _buildSignals({
    required Pop pop,
    required List<PopIdentityBadgeData> identityBadges,
  }) {
    final signals = <AtlasCollectibilitySignal>[];
    final addedIds = <String>{};

    void addSignal(AtlasCollectibilitySignal signal) {
      if (addedIds.add(signal.id)) {
        signals.add(signal);
      }
    }

    for (final badge in identityBadges) {
      addSignal(
        AtlasCollectibilitySignal(
          id: 'identity-${badge.id}',
          label: badge.label,
          description:
          '${badge.displayLabel} is part of this collectible’s release identity.',
          category: 'Identity',
          points: 6,
        ),
      );
    }

    final variantText = _combinedText([
      pop.edition,
      pop.variant,
      pop.finish,
    ]);

    if (_containsAny(variantText, const ['chase'])) {
      addSignal(
        const AtlasCollectibilitySignal(
          id: 'chase',
          label: 'Chase Release',
          description:
          'Chase releases are distinct collectible variants with stronger collector differentiation.',
          category: 'Variant',
          points: 15,
        ),
      );
    }

    if (_containsAny(
      variantText,
      const [
        'prototype',
        'proto',
      ],
    )) {
      addSignal(
        const AtlasCollectibilitySignal(
          id: 'prototype',
          label: 'Prototype',
          description:
          'Prototype collectibles may have unusual production history and limited availability.',
          category: 'Production',
          points: 20,
        ),
      );
    }

    if (_containsAny(
      variantText,
      const [
        'glow',
        'glow in the dark',
        'gitd',
        'flocked',
        'metallic',
        'diamond',
        'blacklight',
        'black light',
        'chrome',
        'translucent',
        'holographic',
      ],
    )) {
      addSignal(
        const AtlasCollectibilitySignal(
          id: 'special-finish',
          label: 'Special Finish',
          description:
          'A special finish gives this release a visually distinct collector identity.',
          category: 'Variant',
          points: 8,
        ),
      );
    }

    if (_hasMeaningfulValue(pop.retailExclusive)) {
      addSignal(
        AtlasCollectibilitySignal(
          id: 'retail-exclusive',
          label: 'Retail Exclusive',
          description:
          'This release is associated with ${pop.retailExclusive.trim()}.',
          category: 'Distribution',
          points: 8,
        ),
      );
    }

    if (_hasMeaningfulValue(pop.eventSticker) ||
        _hasMeaningfulValue(pop.stickerType)) {
      final stickerLabel = _firstMeaningfulValue([
        pop.eventSticker,
        pop.stickerType,
      ]);

      addSignal(
        AtlasCollectibilitySignal(
          id: 'sticker-release',
          label: 'Sticker Release',
          description:
          '$stickerLabel helps distinguish this physical release.',
          category: 'Release',
          points: 8,
        ),
      );
    }

    if (_hasMeaningfulValue(pop.limitedEdition)) {
      addSignal(
        AtlasCollectibilitySignal(
          id: 'limited-edition',
          label: 'Limited Edition',
          description:
          '${pop.limitedEdition.trim()} indicates restricted or numbered production.',
          category: 'Production',
          points: 15,
        ),
      );
    }

    if (_hasMeaningfulSignature(pop.signature)) {
      addSignal(
        AtlasCollectibilitySignal(
          id: 'signed',
          label: 'Signed Collectible',
          description:
          '${pop.signature.trim()} adds autograph-related collector significance.',
          category: 'Authentication',
          points: 10,
        ),
      );
    }

    if (_hasMeaningfulValue(pop.authenticationProvider) ||
        _hasMeaningfulValue(pop.authenticityNumber)) {
      addSignal(
        const AtlasCollectibilitySignal(
          id: 'authenticated',
          label: 'Authentication Recorded',
          description:
          'Authentication information strengthens the collectible’s documented provenance.',
          category: 'Authentication',
          points: 10,
        ),
      );
    }

    if (_hasMeaningfulValue(pop.gradingCompany)) {
      addSignal(
        AtlasCollectibilitySignal(
          id: 'graded',
          label: 'Grading Recorded',
          description:
          '${pop.gradingCompany.trim()} is recorded as the grading company.',
          category: 'Condition',
          points: 8,
        ),
      );
    }

    final productionStatus = pop.productionStatus.trim().toLowerCase();

    if (_containsAny(
      productionStatus,
      const [
        'vaulted',
        'retired',
        'discontinued',
        'out of production',
      ],
    )) {
      addSignal(
        AtlasCollectibilitySignal(
          id: 'production-ended',
          label: 'Production Ended',
          description:
          '${pop.productionStatus.trim()} may increase collector interest by limiting future supply.',
          category: 'Production',
          points: 10,
        ),
      );
    }

    final sourceMetadataCount = [
      pop.sourceCatalogId,
      pop.sourceCollectibleId,
      pop.sourceUpc,
    ].where(_hasMeaningfulValue).length;

    if (sourceMetadataCount >= 2) {
      addSignal(
        const AtlasCollectibilitySignal(
          id: 'catalog-identity',
          label: 'Strong Catalog Identity',
          description:
          'Multiple catalog identifiers strengthen release matching and record confidence.',
          category: 'Data Quality',
          points: 4,
        ),
      );
    }

    return signals;
  }

  List<String> _buildStrengths({
    required AtlasHeroSummary heroSummary,
    required List<AtlasCollectibilitySignal> signals,
  }) {
    final strengths = <String>{};

    for (final reason in heroSummary.scoreReasons) {
      final cleaned = reason.trim();

      if (cleaned.isNotEmpty) {
        strengths.add(cleaned);
      }
    }

    for (final signal in signals) {
      strengths.add(signal.description);
    }

    return strengths.toList(growable: false);
  }

  List<String> _buildOpportunities(Pop pop) {
    final opportunities = <String>[];

    if (pop.value <= 0) {
      opportunities.add(
        'Add a current estimated value to strengthen value and insurance analysis.',
      );
    }

    if (pop.imageUrl.trim().isEmpty) {
      opportunities.add(
        'Add a collectible image to improve identification and presentation.',
      );
    }

    if (pop.releaseDate.trim().isEmpty) {
      opportunities.add(
        'Add the release date to strengthen historical context.',
      );
    }

    if (pop.sourceCatalogId.trim().isEmpty &&
        pop.sourceCollectibleId.trim().isEmpty &&
        pop.sourceUpc.trim().isEmpty) {
      opportunities.add(
        'Add a catalog ID or UPC to strengthen release matching.',
      );
    }

    if (pop.itemCondition.trim().isEmpty) {
      opportunities.add(
        'Record the item condition for stronger collector analysis.',
      );
    }

    if (pop.boxCondition.trim().isEmpty) {
      opportunities.add(
        'Record the box condition for stronger value and display analysis.',
      );
    }

    if (_hasMeaningfulSignature(pop.signature) &&
        !_hasMeaningfulValue(pop.authenticationProvider) &&
        !_hasMeaningfulValue(pop.authenticityNumber)) {
      opportunities.add(
        'Add autograph authentication details when available.',
      );
    }

    return opportunities;
  }

  String _buildConfidence({
    required Pop pop,
    required int signalCount,
  }) {
    var evidenceCount = signalCount;

    if (pop.value > 0) {
      evidenceCount++;
    }

    if (pop.imageUrl.trim().isNotEmpty) {
      evidenceCount++;
    }

    if (pop.releaseDate.trim().isNotEmpty) {
      evidenceCount++;
    }

    if (pop.sourceCatalogId.trim().isNotEmpty ||
        pop.sourceCollectibleId.trim().isNotEmpty ||
        pop.sourceUpc.trim().isNotEmpty) {
      evidenceCount++;
    }

    if (pop.itemCondition.trim().isNotEmpty) {
      evidenceCount++;
    }

    if (pop.boxCondition.trim().isNotEmpty) {
      evidenceCount++;
    }

    if (evidenceCount >= 7) {
      return 'High';
    }

    if (evidenceCount >= 4) {
      return 'Moderate';
    }

    return 'Developing';
  }

  String _buildHeadline({
    required Pop pop,
    required String tier,
    required List<AtlasCollectibilitySignal> signals,
  }) {
    final name = pop.name.trim().isEmpty
        ? 'This collectible'
        : pop.name.trim();

    if (signals.isEmpty) {
      return '$name currently has a standard collector profile.';
    }

    final strongestSignal = signals.reduce(
          (current, next) =>
      next.points > current.points ? next : current,
    );

    return '$name is classified as a $tier, led by its '
        '${strongestSignal.label.toLowerCase()} identity.';
  }

  String _buildExplanation({
    required String tier,
    required String confidence,
    required List<AtlasCollectibilitySignal> signals,
  }) {
    if (signals.isEmpty) {
      return 'Atlas currently classifies this as a $tier. '
          'Additional release, value, condition, and catalog details can '
          'strengthen the analysis. Confidence is $confidence.';
    }

    final strongSignalCount = signals
        .where((signal) => signal.isStrongSignal)
        .length;

    return 'Atlas identified ${signals.length} meaningful collector '
        '${signals.length == 1 ? 'signal' : 'signals'}, including '
        '$strongSignalCount strong '
        '${strongSignalCount == 1 ? 'signal' : 'signals'}. '
        'The current classification is $tier with $confidence confidence.';
  }

  bool _isDisplayPiece({
    required int score,
    required List<AtlasCollectibilitySignal> signals,
  }) {
    final hasVisualSignal = signals.any(
          (signal) =>
      signal.id == 'special-finish' ||
          signal.id == 'chase' ||
          signal.id == 'prototype' ||
          signal.id == 'signed' ||
          signal.id == 'sticker-release',
    );

    return score >= 55 || hasVisualSignal;
  }

  bool _shouldReviewForInsurance({
    required Pop pop,
    required int score,
    required List<AtlasCollectibilitySignal> signals,
  }) {
    final hasStrongDocumentation = signals.any(
          (signal) =>
      signal.id == 'authenticated' ||
          signal.id == 'graded' ||
          signal.id == 'limited-edition' ||
          signal.id == 'prototype',
    );

    return pop.value >= 250 ||
        score >= 90 ||
        (pop.value >= 100 && hasStrongDocumentation);
  }

  String _buildMarketReadiness(Pop pop) {
    final hasValue = pop.value > 0;
    final hasImage = pop.imageUrl.trim().isNotEmpty;
    final hasCondition = pop.itemCondition.trim().isNotEmpty;
    final hasBoxCondition = pop.boxCondition.trim().isNotEmpty;
    final hasReleaseIdentity =
        pop.sourceCatalogId.trim().isNotEmpty ||
            pop.sourceCollectibleId.trim().isNotEmpty ||
            pop.sourceUpc.trim().isNotEmpty;

    if (hasValue &&
        hasImage &&
        hasCondition &&
        hasBoxCondition &&
        hasReleaseIdentity) {
      return 'Ready';
    }

    if (hasValue && (hasImage || hasReleaseIdentity)) {
      return 'Nearly Ready';
    }

    return 'Needs Details';
  }

  static bool _hasMeaningfulValue(String value) {
    final cleaned = value.trim().toLowerCase();

    return cleaned.isNotEmpty &&
        cleaned != 'none' &&
        cleaned != 'no' &&
        cleaned != 'n/a' &&
        cleaned != 'na' &&
        cleaned != 'common' &&
        cleaned != 'standard' &&
        cleaned != 'unsigned';
  }

  static bool _hasMeaningfulSignature(String value) {
    final cleaned = value.trim().toLowerCase();

    return cleaned.isNotEmpty &&
        cleaned != 'none' &&
        cleaned != 'no' &&
        cleaned != 'n/a' &&
        cleaned != 'na' &&
        cleaned != 'unsigned';
  }

  static String _combinedText(List<String> values) {
    return values
        .map((value) => value.trim().toLowerCase())
        .where((value) => value.isNotEmpty)
        .join(' ');
  }

  static bool _containsAny(
      String source,
      List<String> values,
      ) {
    return values.any(source.contains);
  }

  static String _firstMeaningfulValue(List<String> values) {
    for (final value in values) {
      if (_hasMeaningfulValue(value)) {
        return value.trim();
      }
    }

    return 'Recorded sticker information';
  }
}

FILE: .\lib\atlas\recommendations\engine\atlas_recommendation_engine.dart

import '../../../models/pop.dart';
import '../../collectibility/models/atlas_collectibility_summary.dart';
import '../models/atlas_recommendation.dart';
import '../models/atlas_recommendation_priority.dart';
import '../models/atlas_recommendation_summary.dart';
import '../models/atlas_recommendation_type.dart';

/// Produces trusted, actionable next steps for one collectible.
///
/// The engine does not perform navigation or modify collectible data.
/// It only explains what Atlas recommends and why.
class AtlasRecommendationEngine {
  const AtlasRecommendationEngine();

  AtlasRecommendationSummary build({
    required Pop pop,
    required AtlasCollectibilitySummary collectibilitySummary,
  }) {
    final recommendations = <AtlasRecommendation>[];
    final addedIds = <String>{};

    void addRecommendation(
        AtlasRecommendation recommendation,
        ) {
      if (addedIds.add(recommendation.id)) {
        recommendations.add(recommendation);
      }
    }

    _addValueRecommendation(
      pop: pop,
      addRecommendation: addRecommendation,
    );

    if (pop.imageUrl.trim().isEmpty) {
      addRecommendation(
        const AtlasRecommendation(
          id: 'add-image',
          type: AtlasRecommendationType.addImage,
          priority: AtlasRecommendationPriority.medium,
          title: 'Add a collectible image',
          description:
          'Photograph or add an image of this collectible.',
          reason:
          'An image improves identification, presentation, and future comparison workflows.',
          actionLabel: 'Add Image',
        ),
      );
    }

    if (pop.releaseDate.trim().isEmpty) {
      addRecommendation(
        const AtlasRecommendation(
          id: 'add-release-date',
          type: AtlasRecommendationType.addReleaseDate,
          priority: AtlasRecommendationPriority.low,
          title: 'Add the release date',
          description:
          'Record when this collectible was originally released.',
          reason:
          'Release dates improve historical context and future age-based intelligence.',
          actionLabel: 'Add Release Date',
        ),
      );
    }

    if (!_hasCatalogIdentity(pop)) {
      addRecommendation(
        const AtlasRecommendation(
          id: 'add-catalog-identity',
          type: AtlasRecommendationType.addCatalogIdentity,
          priority: AtlasRecommendationPriority.high,
          title: 'Add catalog identification',
          description:
          'Add a UPC, source catalog ID, or collectible ID.',
          reason:
          'Catalog identifiers strengthen duplicate review, release matching, and import accuracy.',
          actionLabel: 'Add Catalog Details',
        ),
      );
    }

    if (pop.itemCondition.trim().isEmpty) {
      addRecommendation(
        const AtlasRecommendation(
          id: 'add-item-condition',
          type: AtlasRecommendationType.addItemCondition,
          priority: AtlasRecommendationPriority.medium,
          title: 'Record item condition',
          description:
          'Document the physical condition of the collectible.',
          reason:
          'Condition is important for value, display, sale, and insurance analysis.',
          actionLabel: 'Add Item Condition',
        ),
      );
    }

    if (pop.boxCondition.trim().isEmpty) {
      addRecommendation(
        const AtlasRecommendation(
          id: 'add-box-condition',
          type: AtlasRecommendationType.addBoxCondition,
          priority: AtlasRecommendationPriority.medium,
          title: 'Record box condition',
          description:
          'Document the current condition of the packaging.',
          reason:
          'Box condition can materially affect collector interest and estimated value.',
          actionLabel: 'Add Box Condition',
        ),
      );
    }

    if (_hasMeaningfulSignature(pop.signature) &&
        !_hasAuthenticationDetails(pop)) {
      addRecommendation(
        const AtlasRecommendation(
          id: 'authenticate-signature',
          type:
          AtlasRecommendationType.authenticateSignature,
          priority: AtlasRecommendationPriority.high,
          title: 'Add autograph authentication',
          description:
          'Record the authentication provider or certificate number.',
          reason:
          'Authentication documentation strengthens confidence in a signed collectible.',
          actionLabel: 'Add Authentication',
        ),
      );
    }

    if (collectibilitySummary.shouldReviewForInsurance) {
      addRecommendation(
        AtlasRecommendation(
          id: 'review-for-insurance',
          type:
          AtlasRecommendationType.reviewForInsurance,
          priority: pop.value >= 500
              ? AtlasRecommendationPriority.high
              : AtlasRecommendationPriority.medium,
          title: 'Review insurance documentation',
          description:
          'Consider whether this collectible should be included in your insured collection records.',
          reason:
          'Its value, score, or documented collector traits make it worth reviewing.',
          actionLabel: 'Review Documentation',
        ),
      );
    }

    if (collectibilitySummary.isDisplayPiece) {
      addRecommendation(
        const AtlasRecommendation(
          id: 'display-collectible',
          type:
          AtlasRecommendationType.displayCollectible,
          priority: AtlasRecommendationPriority.low,
          title: 'Consider this as a display piece',
          description:
          'This collectible has visual or collector traits that may make it suitable for display.',
          reason:
          'Atlas detected a strong score or visually distinctive release identity.',
          actionLabel: 'Review Display Details',
        ),
      );
    }

    if (!collectibilitySummary.isMarketReady) {
      addRecommendation(
        AtlasRecommendation(
          id: 'improve-market-readiness',
          type:
          AtlasRecommendationType.improveMarketReadiness,
          priority:
          collectibilitySummary.marketReadiness ==
              'Needs Details'
              ? AtlasRecommendationPriority.high
              : AtlasRecommendationPriority.medium,
          title: 'Improve market readiness',
          description:
          'Complete the missing value, image, identity, and condition information.',
          reason:
          'Complete records make future sale, comparison, valuation, and insurance workflows more reliable.',
          actionLabel: 'Complete Record',
        ),
      );
    }

    if (_shouldPreserveDocumentation(
      pop: pop,
      collectibilitySummary:
      collectibilitySummary,
    )) {
      addRecommendation(
        const AtlasRecommendation(
          id: 'preserve-documentation',
          type:
          AtlasRecommendationType.preserveDocumentation,
          priority: AtlasRecommendationPriority.medium,
          title: 'Preserve supporting documentation',
          description:
          'Keep certificates, receipts, grading records, and related provenance together.',
          reason:
          'Documented provenance strengthens future authentication, insurance, and sale records.',
          actionLabel: 'Review Documentation',
        ),
      );
    }

    recommendations.sort(_compareRecommendations);

    return AtlasRecommendationSummary(
      recommendations:
      List<AtlasRecommendation>.unmodifiable(
        recommendations,
      ),
    );
  }

  void _addValueRecommendation({
    required Pop pop,
    required void Function(
        AtlasRecommendation recommendation,
        ) addRecommendation,
  }) {
    if (pop.value <= 0) {
      addRecommendation(
        const AtlasRecommendation(
          id: 'add-current-value',
          type:
          AtlasRecommendationType.addCurrentValue,
          priority: AtlasRecommendationPriority.high,
          title: 'Add a current estimated value',
          description:
          'Record the latest estimated value for this collectible.',
          reason:
          'Value is required for collection totals, insurance review, trends, and market analysis.',
          actionLabel: 'Add Current Value',
        ),
      );

      return;
    }

    if (_isValuePossiblyStale(pop)) {
      addRecommendation(
        const AtlasRecommendation(
          id: 'update-current-value',
          type:
          AtlasRecommendationType.updateCurrentValue,
          priority: AtlasRecommendationPriority.medium,
          title: 'Review the current value',
          description:
          'Confirm that the saved estimated value is still current.',
          reason:
          'The recorded value may be outdated based on the last update date.',
          actionLabel: 'Update Current Value',
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  bool _isValuePossiblyStale(Pop pop) {
    final parsedDate =
    DateTime.tryParse(pop.lastUpdated.trim());

    if (parsedDate == null) {
      return true;
    }

    return DateTime.now().difference(parsedDate).inDays >=
        180;
  }

  bool _hasCatalogIdentity(Pop pop) {
    return pop.sourceCatalogId.trim().isNotEmpty ||
        pop.sourceCollectibleId.trim().isNotEmpty ||
        pop.sourceUpc.trim().isNotEmpty;
  }

  bool _hasAuthenticationDetails(Pop pop) {
    return _hasMeaningfulValue(
      pop.authenticationProvider,
    ) ||
        _hasMeaningfulValue(pop.authenticityNumber);
  }

  bool _shouldPreserveDocumentation({
    required Pop pop,
    required AtlasCollectibilitySummary
    collectibilitySummary,
  }) {
    return _hasAuthenticationDetails(pop) ||
        _hasMeaningfulValue(pop.gradingCompany) ||
        _hasMeaningfulSignature(pop.signature) ||
        collectibilitySummary.shouldReviewForInsurance;
  }

  bool _hasMeaningfulSignature(String value) {
    final cleaned = value.trim().toLowerCase();

    return cleaned.isNotEmpty &&
        cleaned != 'none' &&
        cleaned != 'no' &&
        cleaned != 'na' &&
        cleaned != 'n/a' &&
        cleaned != 'unsigned';
  }

  bool _hasMeaningfulValue(String value) {
    final cleaned = value.trim().toLowerCase();

    return cleaned.isNotEmpty &&
        cleaned != 'none' &&
        cleaned != 'no' &&
        cleaned != 'na' &&
        cleaned != 'n/a' &&
        cleaned != 'common' &&
        cleaned != 'standard' &&
        cleaned != 'unsigned';
  }

  int _compareRecommendations(
      AtlasRecommendation first,
      AtlasRecommendation second,
      ) {
    final priorityComparison =
    second.priority.weight.compareTo(
      first.priority.weight,
    );

    if (priorityComparison != 0) {
      return priorityComparison;
    }

    return first.title.compareTo(second.title);
  }
}

import '../atlas/market/models/value_history_entry.dart';
import '../atlas/timeline/models/atlas_timeline_event.dart';

class Pop {
  final String id;

  final String name;
  final String number;

  final String category;
  final String series;

  final String edition;
  final String variant;
  final String retailExclusive;
  final String eventSticker;
  final String limitedEdition;
  final String signature;

  // External catalog identity.
  //
  // For HobbyDB imports:
  // sourceCatalogId = HDBID
  // sourceCollectibleId = HDBC Number
  // sourceUpc = UPC
  final String sourceCatalogId;
  final String sourceCollectibleId;
  final String sourceUpc;

  // Additional source metadata that helps Atlas preserve and explain
  // imported collectible releases.
  final String productionStatus;
  final String releaseDate;
  final String gradingCompany;
  final String authenticityNumber;

  // Physical release identity metadata.
  //
  // These fields describe meaningful release or packaging differences.
  // They do not describe the collector's individual copy condition.
  final String finish;
  final String size;
  final String packagingType;
  final String boxVersion;
  final String copyrightYear;
  final String productionCode;
  final String stickerType;
  final String authenticationProvider;

  final double value;
  final double pricePaid;

  // Chronological estimated-value records for this collectible.
  //
  // Legacy Pops may begin with a single synthesized history entry based on
  // their existing [value] and last known update date.
  final List<ValueHistoryEntry> valueHistory;

  // Chronological collector and market events for this collectible.
  final List<AtlasTimelineEvent> timeline;

  final int quantity;

  final String itemCondition;
  final String boxCondition;

  final String imageUrl;

  final String owner;

  final bool isWishlist;

  final String dateAdded;
  final String lastUpdated;

  const Pop({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.number,
    required this.category,
    required this.series,
    this.edition = 'Common',
    this.variant = 'Common',
    this.retailExclusive = 'None',
    this.eventSticker = 'None',
    this.limitedEdition = 'None',
    this.signature = 'Unsigned',
    this.sourceCatalogId = '',
    this.sourceCollectibleId = '',
    this.sourceUpc = '',
    this.productionStatus = '',
    this.releaseDate = '',
    this.gradingCompany = '',
    this.authenticityNumber = '',
    this.finish = 'Standard',
    this.size = 'Standard 4 Inch',
    this.packagingType = 'Single',
    this.boxVersion = '',
    this.copyrightYear = '',
    this.productionCode = '',
    this.stickerType = '',
    this.authenticationProvider = '',
    required this.value,
    required this.pricePaid,
    this.valueHistory = const [],
    this.timeline = const [],
    required this.quantity,
    required this.itemCondition,
    required this.boxCondition,
    required this.imageUrl,
    required this.owner,
    required this.isWishlist,
    this.dateAdded = '',
    this.lastUpdated = '',
  });

  factory Pop.fromJson(
      Map<String, dynamic> json,
      ) {
    final parsedId = json['id']?.toString() ?? '';
    final legacyEdition = json['edition'] ?? 'Common';

    final parsedValue = _readDouble(json['value']);
    final parsedDateAdded = json['dateAdded']?.toString() ?? '';
    final parsedLastUpdated = json['lastUpdated']?.toString() ?? '';

    final parsedValueHistory = _readValueHistory(
      json['valueHistory'],
      currentValue: parsedValue,
      dateAdded: parsedDateAdded,
      lastUpdated: parsedLastUpdated,
    );

    final parsedTimeline = _readTimeline(
      json['timeline'],
      popId: parsedId,
      dateAdded: parsedDateAdded,
    );

    return Pop(
      id: parsedId,
      name: json['name'] ?? '',
      number: json['number'] ?? '',
      category: json['category'] ?? '',
      series: json['series'] ?? '',
      edition: legacyEdition,
      variant: json['variant'] ?? legacyEdition,
      retailExclusive: json['retailExclusive'] ?? 'None',
      eventSticker: json['eventSticker'] ?? 'None',
      limitedEdition: json['limitedEdition'] ?? 'None',
      signature: json['signature'] ?? 'Unsigned',
      sourceCatalogId: json['sourceCatalogId'] ?? '',
      sourceCollectibleId: json['sourceCollectibleId'] ?? '',
      sourceUpc: json['sourceUpc'] ?? '',
      productionStatus: json['productionStatus'] ?? '',
      releaseDate: json['releaseDate'] ?? '',
      gradingCompany: json['gradingCompany'] ?? '',
      authenticityNumber: json['authenticityNumber'] ?? '',
      finish: json['finish'] ?? 'Standard',
      size: json['size'] ?? 'Standard 4 Inch',
      packagingType: json['packagingType'] ?? 'Single',
      boxVersion: json['boxVersion'] ?? '',
      copyrightYear: json['copyrightYear'] ?? '',
      productionCode: json['productionCode'] ?? '',
      stickerType: json['stickerType'] ?? '',
      authenticationProvider:
      json['authenticationProvider'] ?? '',
      value: parsedValue,
      pricePaid: _readDouble(json['pricePaid']),
      valueHistory: parsedValueHistory,
      timeline: parsedTimeline,
      quantity: _readInt(json['quantity'], fallback: 1),
      itemCondition: json['itemCondition'] ?? '',
      boxCondition: json['boxCondition'] ?? '',
      imageUrl: json['imageUrl'] ?? '',
      owner: json['owner'] ?? 'Shared',
      isWishlist: json['isWishlist'] ?? false,
      dateAdded: parsedDateAdded,
      lastUpdated: parsedLastUpdated,
    );
  }

  /// Identifies the exact collectible release itself for CONFIRMED duplicates.
  ///
  /// No single external identifier is trusted by itself.
  ///
  /// Catalog IDs and UPCs are strong identity evidence, but Funko releases may
  /// reuse UPCs or third-party catalogs may group multiple physical variants
  /// under one record. Release-defining attributes are therefore always included.
  String get collectibleIdentityKey {
    final catalogId = normalizeIdentityText(sourceCatalogId);
    final collectibleId = normalizeIdentityText(sourceCollectibleId);
    final upc = normalizeIdentityText(sourceUpc);

    final normalizedName = normalizeIdentityText(name);
    final normalizedNumber = normalizeIdentityText(number);
    final normalizedCategory = normalizeIdentityText(category);
    final normalizedSeries = normalizeIdentityText(series);

    final identityAnchor = _buildIdentityAnchor(
      catalogId: catalogId,
      collectibleId: collectibleId,
      upc: upc,
      normalizedName: normalizedName,
      normalizedNumber: normalizedNumber,
      normalizedCategory: normalizedCategory,
      normalizedSeries: normalizedSeries,
    );

    if (identityAnchor.isEmpty) {
      return '';
    }

    return <String>[
      'release',
      identityAnchor,
      ..._releaseIdentityParts,
    ].join('|');
  }

  /// Broad identity key used to group POSSIBLE duplicates for review.
  ///
  /// This intentionally ignores release-defining differences such as Chase,
  /// stickers, finishes, signatures, box versions, and production codes.
  ///
  /// Examples:
  ///
  /// Same UPC but Common vs Chase:
  ///   grouped for review, not confirmed as duplicates.
  ///
  /// Same number and series with slightly different names:
  ///   grouped for review.
  ///
  /// Same number in different Funko lines:
  ///   not grouped together.
  String get similarityReviewKey {
    final upc = normalizeIdentityText(sourceUpc);

    if (upc.isNotEmpty) {
      return 'upc_review|$upc';
    }

    final collectibleId = normalizeIdentityText(sourceCollectibleId);

    if (collectibleId.isNotEmpty) {
      return 'collectible_review|$collectibleId';
    }

    final catalogId = normalizeIdentityText(sourceCatalogId);

    if (catalogId.isNotEmpty) {
      return 'catalog_review|$catalogId';
    }

    final normalizedNumber = normalizeIdentityText(number);
    final normalizedCategory = normalizeIdentityText(category);
    final normalizedSeries = normalizeIdentityText(series);
    final normalizedName = normalizeIdentityText(name);

    if (normalizedNumber.isEmpty) {
      return '';
    }

    // Preferred manual review identity:
    // Number + Funko line/category + series.
    //
    // Name is intentionally omitted here so entries such as:
    // "Spider-Man" and "Spider Man"
    // can still be reviewed together.
    if (normalizedCategory.isNotEmpty && normalizedSeries.isNotEmpty) {
      return <String>[
        'line_review',
        normalizedNumber,
        normalizedCategory,
        normalizedSeries,
      ].join('|');
    }

    // When series is missing, retain the name to avoid grouping every #01
    // in a broad category together.
    if (normalizedCategory.isNotEmpty && normalizedName.isNotEmpty) {
      return <String>[
        'category_review',
        normalizedNumber,
        normalizedCategory,
        normalizedName,
      ].join('|');
    }

    // Last-resort review key. This is deliberately conservative.
    if (normalizedSeries.isNotEmpty && normalizedName.isNotEmpty) {
      return <String>[
        'series_review',
        normalizedNumber,
        normalizedSeries,
        normalizedName,
      ].join('|');
    }

    return '';
  }

  /// Builds the strongest available core identity anchor.
  ///
  /// The anchor identifies the probable catalog object. Physical release
  /// attributes are appended separately through [_releaseIdentityParts].
  String _buildIdentityAnchor({
    required String catalogId,
    required String collectibleId,
    required String upc,
    required String normalizedName,
    required String normalizedNumber,
    required String normalizedCategory,
    required String normalizedSeries,
  }) {
    if (collectibleId.isNotEmpty) {
      return 'collectible|$collectibleId';
    }

    if (catalogId.isNotEmpty) {
      return 'catalog|$catalogId';
    }

    if (upc.isNotEmpty) {
      return 'upc|$upc';
    }

    if (normalizedNumber.isEmpty) {
      return '';
    }

    // Number alone is never enough because every Funko line can restart at #01.
    if (normalizedCategory.isEmpty && normalizedSeries.isEmpty) {
      return '';
    }

    return <String>[
      'manual',
      normalizedName,
      normalizedNumber,
      normalizedCategory,
      normalizedSeries,
    ].join('|');
  }

  /// Release-defining attributes used by exact duplicate detection.
  ///
  /// Any meaningful difference here represents either:
  ///
  /// - another variant,
  /// - another sticker release,
  /// - another package format,
  /// - another box version,
  /// - another production run,
  /// - or another authenticated collectible.
  List<String> get _releaseIdentityParts {
    return <String>[
      normalizeVariantIdentity(edition),
      normalizeVariantIdentity(variant),
      normalizeVariantIdentity(finish),
      normalizeOptionalIdentity(retailExclusive),
      normalizeOptionalIdentity(eventSticker),
      normalizeOptionalIdentity(stickerType),
      normalizeOptionalIdentity(limitedEdition),
      normalizeSignatureIdentity(signature),
      normalizeOptionalIdentity(authenticationProvider),
      normalizeOptionalIdentity(gradingCompany),
      normalizeOptionalIdentity(authenticityNumber),
      normalizeOptionalIdentity(size),
      normalizeOptionalIdentity(packagingType),
      normalizeOptionalIdentity(boxVersion),
      normalizeOptionalIdentity(copyrightYear),
      normalizeOptionalIdentity(productionCode),
    ];
  }

  /// Identifies one stored record in a collection.
  ///
  /// Owner and wishlist status remain separate so different collectors and
  /// owned/wishlist records are never merged together.
  String get collectionRecordKey {
    final collectibleKey = collectibleIdentityKey;

    if (collectibleKey.isEmpty) {
      return '';
    }

    return [
      collectibleKey,
      normalizeIdentityText(owner),
      isWishlist ? 'wishlist' : 'owned',
    ].join('|');
  }

  String get csvImportKey => collectionRecordKey;

  String get duplicateKey => collectionRecordKey;

  String get collectorIdentity => collectibleIdentityKey;

  String get collectorLabel {
    final parts = <String>[
      variant,
      retailExclusive,
      eventSticker,
      limitedEdition,
      signature,
    ].where(hasMeaningfulIdentityLabel);

    if (parts.isEmpty) {
      return 'Common';
    }

    return parts.join(' • ');
  }

  Pop copyWith({
    String? id,
    String? name,
    String? number,
    String? category,
    String? series,
    String? edition,
    String? variant,
    String? retailExclusive,
    String? eventSticker,
    String? limitedEdition,
    String? signature,
    String? sourceCatalogId,
    String? sourceCollectibleId,
    String? sourceUpc,
    String? productionStatus,
    String? releaseDate,
    String? gradingCompany,
    String? authenticityNumber,
    String? finish,
    String? size,
    String? packagingType,
    String? boxVersion,
    String? copyrightYear,
    String? productionCode,
    String? stickerType,
    String? authenticationProvider,
    double? value,
    double? pricePaid,
    List<ValueHistoryEntry>? valueHistory,
    List<AtlasTimelineEvent>? timeline,
    int? quantity,
    String? itemCondition,
    String? boxCondition,
    String? imageUrl,
    String? owner,
    bool? isWishlist,
    String? dateAdded,
    String? lastUpdated,
  }) {
    return Pop(
      id: id ?? this.id,
      name: name ?? this.name,
      number: number ?? this.number,
      category: category ?? this.category,
      series: series ?? this.series,
      edition: edition ?? this.edition,
      variant: variant ?? this.variant,
      retailExclusive: retailExclusive ?? this.retailExclusive,
      eventSticker: eventSticker ?? this.eventSticker,
      limitedEdition: limitedEdition ?? this.limitedEdition,
      signature: signature ?? this.signature,
      sourceCatalogId: sourceCatalogId ?? this.sourceCatalogId,
      sourceCollectibleId:
      sourceCollectibleId ?? this.sourceCollectibleId,
      sourceUpc: sourceUpc ?? this.sourceUpc,
      productionStatus: productionStatus ?? this.productionStatus,
      releaseDate: releaseDate ?? this.releaseDate,
      gradingCompany: gradingCompany ?? this.gradingCompany,
      authenticityNumber:
      authenticityNumber ?? this.authenticityNumber,
      finish: finish ?? this.finish,
      size: size ?? this.size,
      packagingType: packagingType ?? this.packagingType,
      boxVersion: boxVersion ?? this.boxVersion,
      copyrightYear: copyrightYear ?? this.copyrightYear,
      productionCode: productionCode ?? this.productionCode,
      stickerType: stickerType ?? this.stickerType,
      authenticationProvider:
      authenticationProvider ?? this.authenticationProvider,
      value: value ?? this.value,
      pricePaid: pricePaid ?? this.pricePaid,
      valueHistory: valueHistory ?? this.valueHistory,
      timeline: timeline ?? this.timeline,
      quantity: quantity ?? this.quantity,
      itemCondition: itemCondition ?? this.itemCondition,
      boxCondition: boxCondition ?? this.boxCondition,
      imageUrl: imageUrl ?? this.imageUrl,
      owner: owner ?? this.owner,
      isWishlist: isWishlist ?? this.isWishlist,
      dateAdded: dateAdded ?? this.dateAdded,
      lastUpdated: lastUpdated ?? this.lastUpdated,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'name': name,
      'number': number,
      'category': category,
      'series': series,
      'edition': edition,
      'variant': variant,
      'retailExclusive': retailExclusive,
      'eventSticker': eventSticker,
      'limitedEdition': limitedEdition,
      'signature': signature,
      'sourceCatalogId': sourceCatalogId,
      'sourceCollectibleId': sourceCollectibleId,
      'sourceUpc': sourceUpc,
      'productionStatus': productionStatus,
      'releaseDate': releaseDate,
      'gradingCompany': gradingCompany,
      'authenticityNumber': authenticityNumber,
      'finish': finish,
      'size': size,
      'packagingType': packagingType,
      'boxVersion': boxVersion,
      'copyrightYear': copyrightYear,
      'productionCode': productionCode,
      'stickerType': stickerType,
      'authenticationProvider': authenticationProvider,
      'value': value,
      'pricePaid': pricePaid,
      'valueHistory': valueHistory
          .map((entry) => entry.toJson())
          .toList(growable: false),
      'timeline': timeline
          .map((event) => event.toJson())
          .toList(growable: false),
      'quantity': quantity,
      'itemCondition': itemCondition,
      'boxCondition': boxCondition,
      'imageUrl': imageUrl,
      'owner': owner,
      'isWishlist': isWishlist,
      'dateAdded': dateAdded,
      'lastUpdated': lastUpdated,
    };
  }

  static double _readDouble(dynamic rawValue) {
    if (rawValue is num) {
      return rawValue.toDouble();
    }

    return double.tryParse(
      rawValue?.toString().trim() ?? '',
    ) ??
        0;
  }

  static int _readInt(
      dynamic rawValue, {
        required int fallback,
      }) {
    if (rawValue is int) {
      return rawValue;
    }

    if (rawValue is num) {
      return rawValue.toInt();
    }

    return int.tryParse(
      rawValue?.toString().trim() ?? '',
    ) ??
        fallback;
  }

  static List<ValueHistoryEntry> _readValueHistory(
      dynamic rawHistory, {
        required double currentValue,
        required String dateAdded,
        required String lastUpdated,
      }) {
    final entries = <ValueHistoryEntry>[];

    if (rawHistory is List) {
      for (final rawEntry in rawHistory) {
        if (rawEntry is! Map) {
          continue;
        }

        final entry = ValueHistoryEntry.fromJson(
          Map<String, dynamic>.from(rawEntry),
        );

        if (entry.value <= 0) {
          continue;
        }

        entries.add(entry);
      }
    }

    if (entries.isNotEmpty) {
      entries.sort(
            (a, b) => a.recordedAt.compareTo(b.recordedAt),
      );

      return List<ValueHistoryEntry>.unmodifiable(entries);
    }

    if (currentValue <= 0) {
      return const [];
    }

    final recordedAt =
        DateTime.tryParse(lastUpdated) ??
            DateTime.tryParse(dateAdded) ??
            DateTime.fromMillisecondsSinceEpoch(0);

    return List<ValueHistoryEntry>.unmodifiable(
      <ValueHistoryEntry>[
        ValueHistoryEntry(
          value: currentValue,
          recordedAt: recordedAt,
          source: ValueHistoryEntry.legacyValueSource,
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  static List<AtlasTimelineEvent> _readTimeline(
      dynamic rawTimeline, {
        required String popId,
        required String dateAdded,
      }) {
    final events = <AtlasTimelineEvent>[];

    if (rawTimeline is List) {
      for (final rawEvent in rawTimeline) {
        if (rawEvent is! Map) {
          continue;
        }

        final event = AtlasTimelineEvent.fromJson(
          Map<String, dynamic>.from(rawEvent),
        );

        events.add(event);
      }
    }

    if (events.isNotEmpty) {
      events.sort(
            (a, b) => a.occurredAt.compareTo(b.occurredAt),
      );

      return List<AtlasTimelineEvent>.unmodifiable(events);
    }

    final addedAt = DateTime.tryParse(dateAdded);

    if (popId.isEmpty || addedAt == null) {
      return const [];
    }

    return List<AtlasTimelineEvent>.unmodifiable(
      <AtlasTimelineEvent>[
        AtlasTimelineEvent(
          id: 'legacy-added-$popId',
          popId: popId,
          type: AtlasTimelineEventType.addedToVault,
          occurredAt: addedAt,
          title: 'Added to Vault',
          createdBy: AtlasTimelineEvent.systemCreatedBy,
          createdAt: addedAt,
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  // Cleans strings by lowercasing and stripping non-alphanumeric characters.
  // Converts "Spider-Man #01!" -> "spiderman01"
  static String normalizeIdentityText(String value) {
    return value
        .trim()
        .toLowerCase()
        .replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^a-z0-9]'), '');
  }

  static String normalizeVariantIdentity(String value) {
    final cleaned = normalizeIdentityText(value);

    if (cleaned.isEmpty ||
        cleaned == 'none' ||
        cleaned == 'no' ||
        cleaned == 'na' ||
        cleaned == 'common' ||
        cleaned == 'standard') {
      return 'common';
    }

    return cleaned;
  }

  static String normalizeOptionalIdentity(String value) {
    final cleaned = normalizeIdentityText(value);

    if (cleaned.isEmpty ||
        cleaned == 'none' ||
        cleaned == 'no' ||
        cleaned == 'na') {
      return 'none';
    }

    return cleaned;
  }

  static String normalizeSignatureIdentity(String value) {
    final cleaned = normalizeIdentityText(value);

    if (cleaned.isEmpty ||
        cleaned == 'none' ||
        cleaned == 'no' ||
        cleaned == 'na' ||
        cleaned == 'unsigned') {
      return 'unsigned';
    }

    return cleaned;
  }

  static bool hasMeaningfulIdentityLabel(String value) {
    final cleaned = normalizeIdentityText(value);

    return cleaned.isNotEmpty &&
        cleaned != 'none' &&
        cleaned != 'no' &&
        cleaned != 'na' &&
        cleaned != 'unsigned' &&
        cleaned != 'common' &&
        cleaned != 'standard';
  }
}

import '../../../models/pop.dart';
import '../converters/atlas_catalog_converter.dart';
import '../models/atlas_catalog_product.dart';

// Central creation point for Vault Pop records.
//
// Screens, scanners, imports, catalog browsing, wishlist flows, and future
// Atlas Intelligence features should use this factory rather than constructing
// Pop records directly.
abstract final class AtlasPopFactory {
  // Creates a completely manual Vault record.
  //
  // This is used when no trusted Atlas catalog product is available.
  static Pop createManual({
    required String name,
    required String number,
    required String category,
    required String series,
    String variant = 'Common',
    String retailExclusive = 'None',
    String eventSticker = 'None',
    String limitedEdition = 'None',
    String signature = 'Unsigned',
    String productionCode = '',
    String copyrightYear = '',
    String stickerType = '',
    String authenticationProvider = '',
    String authenticityNumber = '',
    double value = 0,
    double pricePaid = 0,
    int quantity = 1,
    String itemCondition = '',
    String boxCondition = '',
    String imageUrl = '',
    String owner = 'Shared',
    bool isWishlist = false,
    DateTime? timestamp,
  }) {
    final DateTime resolvedTimestamp = timestamp ?? DateTime.now();

    final String now = resolvedTimestamp.toIso8601String();

    final String resolvedVariant = _defaultText(variant, fallback: 'Common');

    return Pop(
      id: resolvedTimestamp.microsecondsSinceEpoch.toString(),
      name: name.trim(),
      number: number.trim(),
      category: category.trim(),
      series: series.trim(),
      edition: resolvedVariant,
      variant: resolvedVariant,
      retailExclusive: _defaultText(retailExclusive, fallback: 'None'),
      eventSticker: _defaultText(eventSticker, fallback: 'None'),
      limitedEdition: _defaultText(limitedEdition, fallback: 'None'),
      signature: _defaultText(signature, fallback: 'Unsigned'),
      sourceCatalogId: '',
      sourceCollectibleId: '',
      sourceUpc: '',
      productionStatus: '',
      releaseDate: '',
      gradingCompany: '',
      productionCode: productionCode.trim(),
      copyrightYear: copyrightYear.trim(),
      stickerType: stickerType.trim(),
      authenticationProvider: authenticationProvider.trim(),
      authenticityNumber: authenticityNumber.trim(),
      value: value < 0 ? 0 : value,
      pricePaid: pricePaid < 0 ? 0 : pricePaid,
      quantity: quantity < 1 ? 1 : quantity,
      itemCondition: itemCondition.trim(),
      boxCondition: boxCondition.trim(),
      imageUrl: imageUrl.trim(),
      owner: _defaultText(owner, fallback: 'Shared'),
      isWishlist: isWishlist,
      dateAdded: now,
      lastUpdated: now,
    );
  }

  // Creates a Vault record from a trusted Atlas catalog product.
  //
  // The converter supplies catalog identity, barcode, image, value, and other
  // trusted metadata. Optional overrides allow the user to edit the form
  // before saving without losing the catalog connection.
  static Pop createFromCatalog({
    required AtlasCatalogProduct product,
    String? name,
    String? number,
    String? category,
    String? series,
    String? variant,
    String? retailExclusive,
    String? eventSticker,
    String limitedEdition = 'None',
    String? signature,
    String? productionCode,
    String? copyrightYear,
    String? stickerType,
    String? authenticationProvider,
    String? authenticityNumber,
    double? value,
    double pricePaid = 0,
    int quantity = 1,
    String itemCondition = '',
    String boxCondition = '',
    String? imageUrl,
    String owner = 'Shared',
    bool isWishlist = false,
    DateTime? timestamp,
  }) {
    final Pop catalogPop = AtlasCatalogConverter.toPop(
      product,
      owner: owner,
      isWishlist: isWishlist,
      quantity: quantity,
      pricePaid: pricePaid,
      itemCondition: itemCondition,
      boxCondition: boxCondition,
      timestamp: timestamp,
    );

    final String resolvedVariant = _defaultText(
      variant ?? catalogPop.variant,
      fallback: 'Common',
    );

    return catalogPop.copyWith(
      name: name?.trim() ?? catalogPop.name,
      number: number?.trim() ?? catalogPop.number,
      category: category?.trim() ?? catalogPop.category,
      series: series?.trim() ?? catalogPop.series,
      edition: resolvedVariant,
      variant: resolvedVariant,
      retailExclusive: retailExclusive == null
          ? catalogPop.retailExclusive
          : _defaultText(retailExclusive, fallback: 'None'),
      eventSticker: eventSticker == null
          ? catalogPop.eventSticker
          : _defaultText(eventSticker, fallback: 'None'),
      limitedEdition: _resolveCatalogIdentity(
        selectedValue: limitedEdition,
        catalogValue: catalogPop.limitedEdition,
        fallback: 'None',
      ),
      signature: signature == null
          ? catalogPop.signature
          : _defaultText(signature, fallback: 'Unsigned'),
      productionCode: productionCode?.trim() ?? catalogPop.productionCode,
      copyrightYear: copyrightYear?.trim() ?? catalogPop.copyrightYear,
      stickerType: stickerType?.trim() ?? catalogPop.stickerType,
      authenticationProvider:
          authenticationProvider?.trim() ?? catalogPop.authenticationProvider,
      authenticityNumber:
          authenticityNumber?.trim() ?? catalogPop.authenticityNumber,
      value: value == null
          ? catalogPop.value
          : value < 0
          ? 0
          : value,
      pricePaid: pricePaid < 0 ? 0 : pricePaid,
      quantity: quantity < 1 ? 1 : quantity,
      itemCondition: itemCondition.trim(),
      boxCondition: boxCondition.trim(),
      imageUrl: imageUrl?.trim() ?? catalogPop.imageUrl,
      owner: _defaultText(owner, fallback: 'Shared'),
      isWishlist: isWishlist,
    );
  }

  // Refreshes trusted catalog metadata on an existing Vault record.
  //
  // Personal ownership information remains protected by the converter.
  static Pop refreshFromCatalog({
    required Pop existingPop,
    required AtlasCatalogProduct product,
    DateTime? timestamp,
  }) {
    return AtlasCatalogConverter.applyCatalogMetadata(
      existingPop: existingPop,
      product: product,
      timestamp: timestamp,
    );
  }

  // Creates a wishlist record from a trusted Atlas catalog product.
  static Pop createWishlistFromCatalog({
    required AtlasCatalogProduct product,
    String owner = 'Shared',
    DateTime? timestamp,
  }) {
    return createFromCatalog(
      product: product,
      owner: owner,
      isWishlist: true,
      quantity: 1,
      timestamp: timestamp,
    );
  }

  static String _defaultText(String value, {required String fallback}) {
    final String normalized = value.trim();

    if (normalized.isEmpty) {
      return fallback;
    }

    return normalized;
  }

  static String _resolveCatalogIdentity({
    required String selectedValue,
    required String catalogValue,
    required String fallback,
  }) {
    final String normalizedSelection = selectedValue.trim();

    final bool selectionIsDefault =
        normalizedSelection.isEmpty ||
        normalizedSelection.toLowerCase() == 'none';

    if (!selectionIsDefault) {
      return normalizedSelection;
    }

    final String normalizedCatalogValue = catalogValue.trim();

    if (normalizedCatalogValue.isNotEmpty &&
        normalizedCatalogValue.toLowerCase() != 'none') {
      return normalizedCatalogValue;
    }

    return fallback;
  }
}
