import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

import 'package:funkovault2/atlas/catalog/models/atlas_catalog_product.dart';
import 'package:funkovault2/atlas/catalog/services/atlas_pop_factory.dart';
import 'package:funkovault2/atlas/intelligence/engines/atlas_collector_insight_engine.dart';
import 'package:funkovault2/atlas/scan/acquisition/atlas_continuous_scan_coordinator.dart';
import 'package:funkovault2/atlas/scan/ledger/atlas_scan_ledger_entry.dart';
import 'package:funkovault2/atlas/scan/results/atlas_continuous_scan_result.dart';
import 'package:funkovault2/screens/scanner/atlas_visual_inspection_screen.dart';
import 'package:funkovault2/models/pop.dart';
import 'package:funkovault2/screens/pop/add_pop_screen.dart';
import 'package:funkovault2/services/app_state.dart';
import 'package:funkovault2/theme/atlas_colors.dart';
import 'package:funkovault2/theme/atlas_spacing.dart';
import 'package:funkovault2/widgets/atlas/atlas_glass_card.dart';
import 'package:funkovault2/widgets/atlas/components/atlas_section_header.dart';
import 'package:funkovault2/widgets/atlas/components/atlas_status_chip.dart';

class AtlasHaulReviewScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const AtlasHaulReviewScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.coordinator,
    required this.collectionName,
  });

  final AtlasContinuousScanCoordinator coordinator;
  final String collectionName;

  @override
  State<AtlasHaulReviewScreen> createState() => _AtlasHaulReviewScreenState();
}

class _AtlasHaulReviewScreenState extends State<AtlasHaulReviewScreen> {
  final Set<String> _savingTrackIds = <String>{};

  Future<void> _inspectItem({
    required AtlasScanLedgerEntry entry,
    required AtlasContinuousScanResult result,
  }) async {
    final session = await Navigator.push(
      context,
      MaterialPageRoute(
        builder: (_) => AtlasVisualInspectionScreen(session: result.session),
      ),
    );
    if (!mounted || session == null) return;
    widget.coordinator.applyInspection(
      trackId: entry.trackId,
      session: session,
    );
    setState(() {});
  }

  Future<bool> _runSaveForTrack({
    required AtlasScanLedgerEntry entry,
    required Widget Function(BuildContext context) screenBuilder,
    required String successLabel,
  }) async {
    if (_savingTrackIds.contains(entry.trackId)) {
      return false;
    }

    setState(() {
      _savingTrackIds.add(entry.trackId);
    });

    try {
      final bool? added = await Navigator.push<bool>(
        context,
        MaterialPageRoute<bool>(builder: screenBuilder),
      );

      if (!mounted || added != true) {
        return false;
      }

      final bool committed = widget.coordinator.markCommitted(
        trackId: entry.trackId,
      );

      if (!committed) {
        return false;
      }

      setState(() {});

      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
        ..hideCurrentSnackBar()
        ..showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: Text(
              '$successLabel committed to ${widget.collectionName}.',
            ),
          ),
        );

      return true;
    } finally {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _savingTrackIds.remove(entry.trackId);
        });
      } else {
        _savingTrackIds.remove(entry.trackId);
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _addCatalogItem({
    required AtlasScanLedgerEntry entry,
    required AtlasCatalogProduct product,
  }) async {
    await _runSaveForTrack(
      entry: entry,
      successLabel: product.displayName,
      screenBuilder: (_) {
        final inspection = widget.coordinator
            .resultForTrack(entry.trackId)
            ?.session
            .visualInspectionContribution
            ?.inspection;
        return AddPopScreen(
          atlasProduct: product,
          collectionName: widget.collectionName,
          visualInspection: inspection,
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Future<void> _addManualItem({required AtlasScanLedgerEntry entry}) async {
    await _runSaveForTrack(
      entry: entry,
      successLabel: 'Reviewed item',
      screenBuilder: (_) {
        final inspection = widget.coordinator
            .resultForTrack(entry.trackId)
            ?.session
            .visualInspectionContribution
            ?.inspection;
        return AddPopScreen(
          collectionName: widget.collectionName,
          visualInspection: inspection,
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Future<void> _linkExistingVaultItem({
    required AtlasScanLedgerEntry entry,
    required List<Pop> collectionPops,
  }) async {
    if (_savingTrackIds.contains(entry.trackId)) {
      return;
    }

    final Pop? selected = await showModalBottomSheet<Pop>(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      builder: (_) {
        return _VaultItemPickerSheet(
          collectionName: widget.collectionName,
          collectionPops: collectionPops,
          scannedBarcode: entry.stableCode.normalizedValue,
        );
      },
    );

    if (!mounted || selected == null) {
      return;
    }

    final String scannedBarcode = entry.stableCode.normalizedValue.trim();
    final String existingBarcode = selected.sourceUpc.trim();

    if (existingBarcode.isNotEmpty && existingBarcode != scannedBarcode) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
        ..hideCurrentSnackBar()
        ..showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: Text(
              '${selected.name} already has barcode $existingBarcode. Atlas did not overwrite it.',
            ),
          ),
        );
      return;
    }

    setState(() {
      _savingTrackIds.add(entry.trackId);
    });

    try {
      final AppState appState = context.read<AppState>();
      final Pop linkedPop = existingBarcode == scannedBarcode
          ? selected
          : selected.copyWith(sourceUpc: scannedBarcode);

      final Pop? persisted = await appState.updatePop(
        linkedPop,
        collectionName: widget.collectionName,
        existingRecordKey: selected.collectionRecordKey,
      );

      if (!mounted) {
        return;
      }

      if (persisted == null) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
          ..hideCurrentSnackBar()
          ..showSnackBar(
            const SnackBar(
              content: Text(
                'Atlas could not safely link that Vault item. Nothing was changed.',
              ),
            ),
          );
        return;
      }

      final bool committed = widget.coordinator.markCommitted(
        trackId: entry.trackId,
      );

      if (!committed) {
        return;
      }

      setState(() {});

      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
        ..hideCurrentSnackBar()
        ..showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: Text(
              'Linked barcode $scannedBarcode to ${persisted.name} and committed this haul item.',
            ),
          ),
        );
    } finally {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _savingTrackIds.remove(entry.trackId);
        });
      } else {
        _savingTrackIds.remove(entry.trackId);
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _reviewCatalogMatches({
    required AtlasScanLedgerEntry entry,
    required AtlasContinuousScanResult result,
  }) async {
    final List<AtlasCatalogProduct> matches = result.catalogResolution.matches;

    if (matches.isEmpty) {
      return;
    }

    final AtlasCatalogProduct?
    selected = await showModalBottomSheet<AtlasCatalogProduct>(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      builder: (BuildContext sheetContext) {
        return SafeArea(
          child: FractionallySizedBox(
            heightFactor: 0.72,
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
              children: [
                Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AtlasSpacing.lg),
                  child: AtlasSectionHeader(
                    title: '🔎 Review Catalog Matches',
                    subtitle:
                        'Choose the exact collectible before adding it to the Vault.',
                    icon: Icons.content_copy_rounded,
                    accentColor: AtlasColors.warning,
                    trailing: AtlasStatusChip(
                      label: '${matches.length} matches',
                      icon: Icons.layers_outlined,
                      accentColor: AtlasColors.warning,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                Expanded(
                  child: ListView.separated(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
                      AtlasSpacing.lg,
                      0,
                      AtlasSpacing.lg,
                      AtlasSpacing.lg,
                    ),
                    itemCount: matches.length,
                    separatorBuilder: (_, _) =>
                        const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.sm),
                    itemBuilder: (BuildContext context, int index) {
                      final AtlasCatalogProduct product = matches[index];

                      return AtlasGlassCard(
                        accentColor: AtlasColors.warning,
                        onTap: () {
                          Navigator.pop(sheetContext, product);
                        },
                        child: Row(
                          children: [
                            const Icon(
                              Icons.inventory_2_outlined,
                              color: AtlasColors.warning,
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(width: AtlasSpacing.md),
                            Expanded(
                              child: Text(
                                product.displayName,
                                style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleSmall
                                    ?.copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700),
                              ),
                            ),
                            const Icon(Icons.chevron_right_rounded),
                          ],
                        ),
                      );
                    },
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    );

    if (!mounted || selected == null) {
      return;
    }

    await _addCatalogItem(entry: entry, product: selected);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final AppState appState = context.watch<AppState>();

    final List<AtlasScanLedgerEntry> entries =
        widget.coordinator.ledger.entries.toList(growable: false)..sort(
          (AtlasScanLedgerEntry a, AtlasScanLedgerEntry b) =>
              b.createdAt.compareTo(a.createdAt),
        );

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('Review Haul')),
      body: SafeArea(
        child: ListView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(AtlasSpacing.md),
          children: [
            AtlasGlassCard(
              accentColor: AtlasColors.vaultGold,
              child: AtlasSectionHeader(
                title: '📦 Review Haul',
                subtitle:
                    'Nothing enters the Vault until you explicitly save it.',
                icon: Icons.fact_check_outlined,
                accentColor: AtlasColors.vaultGold,
                trailing: AtlasStatusChip(
                  label:
                      '${entries.length} item${entries.length == 1 ? '' : 's'}',
                  icon: Icons.inventory_2_outlined,
                  accentColor: AtlasColors.vaultGold,
                ),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.md),
            if (entries.isEmpty)
              AtlasGlassCard(
                child: const Text('No haul items are ready for review yet.'),
              )
            else
              ...entries.map((AtlasScanLedgerEntry entry) {
                final AtlasContinuousScanResult? result = widget.coordinator
                    .resultForTrack(entry.trackId);
                final AtlasCatalogProduct? resolvedProduct =
                    result?.resolvedCatalogProduct;
                final bool hasMultipleMatches =
                    result?.catalogResolution.hasMultipleMatches ?? false;

                return Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AtlasSpacing.md),
                  child: _HaulReviewItemCard(
                    entry: entry,
                    result: result,
                    collectionName: widget.collectionName,
                    collectionPops: appState.popsForCollection(
                      widget.collectionName,
                    ),
                    ownedWishlistIdentityKeys: appState
                        .ownedWishlistIdentityKeysForCollection(
                          widget.collectionName,
                        ),
                    isSaving: _savingTrackIds.contains(entry.trackId),
                    onRetry: entry.hasFailed
                        ? () {
                            final bool accepted = widget.coordinator
                                .retryFailedTrack(trackId: entry.trackId);

                            if (accepted && mounted) {
                              setState(() {});
                            }
                          }
                        : null,
                    onAdd: resolvedProduct == null
                        ? null
                        : () => _addCatalogItem(
                            entry: entry,
                            product: resolvedProduct,
                          ),
                    onReviewMatches: !hasMultipleMatches || result == null
                        ? null
                        : () => _reviewCatalogMatches(
                            entry: entry,
                            result: result,
                          ),
                    onFindExisting:
                        entry.needsReview &&
                            resolvedProduct == null &&
                            !hasMultipleMatches
                        ? () => _linkExistingVaultItem(
                            entry: entry,
                            collectionPops: appState.popsForCollection(
                              widget.collectionName,
                            ),
                          )
                        : null,
                    onManualAdd:
                        entry.needsReview &&
                            resolvedProduct == null &&
                            !hasMultipleMatches
                        ? () => _addManualItem(entry: entry)
                        : null,
                    onInspect: result == null || entry.isCommitted
                        ? null
                        : () => _inspectItem(entry: entry, result: result),
                  ),
                );
              }),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _HaulReviewItemCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const _HaulReviewItemCard({
    required this.entry,
    required this.result,
    required this.collectionName,
    required this.collectionPops,
    required this.ownedWishlistIdentityKeys,
    required this.isSaving,
    required this.onRetry,
    required this.onAdd,
    required this.onReviewMatches,
    required this.onFindExisting,
    required this.onManualAdd,
    required this.onInspect,
  });

  final AtlasScanLedgerEntry entry;
  final AtlasContinuousScanResult? result;
  final String collectionName;
  final List<Pop> collectionPops;
  final Set<String> ownedWishlistIdentityKeys;
  final bool isSaving;
  final VoidCallback? onRetry;
  final VoidCallback? onAdd;
  final VoidCallback? onReviewMatches;
  final VoidCallback? onFindExisting;
  final VoidCallback? onManualAdd;
  final VoidCallback? onInspect;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final AtlasCatalogProduct? catalogProduct = result?.resolvedCatalogProduct;
    final AtlasContinuousScanResult? scanResult = result;

    bool isOwned = false;
    bool isWishlisted = false;
    int ownedCopyCount = 0;
    int relatedMatchCount = 0;

    if (catalogProduct != null && scanResult != null) {
      final Pop productPop = AtlasPopFactory.createFromCatalog(
        product: catalogProduct,
      );

      final insight = const AtlasCollectorInsightEngine().evaluateScan(
        session: scanResult.session,
        product: productPop,
        scannedBarcode: entry.stableCode.normalizedValue,
        collectionPops: collectionPops,
        collectionName: collectionName,
        ownedWishlistIdentityKeys: ownedWishlistIdentityKeys,
      );

      isOwned = insight.state.isOwned;
      isWishlisted = insight.state.isWishlisted;
      ownedCopyCount = insight.state.ownedMatches.fold<int>(
        0,
        (int total, Pop pop) => total + pop.quantity,
      );
      relatedMatchCount = insight.state.relatedMatches.length;
    }

    final Color accentColor = _accentForStatus(entry.status);
    final bool hasMultipleMatches =
        scanResult?.catalogResolution.hasMultipleMatches ?? false;
    final int matchCount = scanResult?.catalogResolution.matches.length ?? 0;

    final String title =
        catalogProduct?.displayName ??
        (hasMultipleMatches
            ? '$matchCount Possible Matches'
            : 'Barcode ${entry.stableCode.normalizedValue}');

    return AtlasGlassCard(
      accentColor: accentColor,
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          AtlasSectionHeader(
            title: title,
            subtitle: _statusMessage(entry),
            icon: _iconForStatus(entry.status),
            accentColor: accentColor,
            trailing: AtlasStatusChip(
              label: _statusLabel(entry.status),
              icon: _iconForStatus(entry.status),
              accentColor: accentColor,
            ),
          ),
          if (catalogProduct != null ||
              scanResult?.isOfflineResult == true ||
              scanResult?.requiresSync == true) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.md),
            Wrap(
              spacing: AtlasSpacing.sm,
              runSpacing: AtlasSpacing.sm,
              children: [
                if (catalogProduct != null) ...[
                  AtlasStatusChip(
                    label: isOwned ? 'Owned ×$ownedCopyCount' : 'Not Owned',
                    icon: isOwned
                        ? Icons.inventory_2_rounded
                        : Icons.inventory_2_outlined,
                    accentColor: isOwned
                        ? AtlasColors.success
                        : AtlasColors.textSecondary,
                  ),
                  AtlasStatusChip(
                    label: isWishlisted ? 'Wishlisted' : 'Not Wishlisted',
                    icon: isWishlisted
                        ? Icons.favorite_rounded
                        : Icons.favorite_border_rounded,
                    accentColor: isWishlisted
                        ? AtlasColors.danger
                        : AtlasColors.textSecondary,
                  ),
                  if (relatedMatchCount > 0)
                    AtlasStatusChip(
                      label:
                          '$relatedMatchCount related variant${relatedMatchCount == 1 ? '' : 's'}',
                      icon: Icons.layers_outlined,
                      accentColor: AtlasColors.warning,
                    ),
                ],
                if (scanResult?.isOfflineResult == true)
                  const AtlasStatusChip(
                    label: 'Offline',
                    icon: Icons.cloud_off_outlined,
                    accentColor: AtlasColors.intelligenceBlue,
                  ),
                if (scanResult?.requiresSync == true)
                  const AtlasStatusChip(
                    label: 'Sync Pending',
                    icon: Icons.cloud_sync_outlined,
                    accentColor: AtlasColors.warning,
                  ),
              ],
            ),
          ],
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.md),
          if (scanResult?.session.authenticityContribution != null) ...[
            AtlasStatusChip(
              label: _authenticityLabel(
                scanResult!.session.authenticityContribution!.assessment.status,
              ),
              icon: Icons.shield_outlined,
              accentColor:
                  scanResult
                      .session
                      .authenticityContribution!
                      .assessment
                      .requiresReview
                  ? AtlasColors.warning
                  : AtlasColors.success,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.sm),
          ],
          if (onInspect != null) ...[
            OutlinedButton.icon(
              onPressed: onInspect,
              icon: const Icon(Icons.verified_user_outlined),
              label: Text(
                scanResult?.session.hasAuthenticityAssessment == true
                    ? '🔎 Review Visual Inspection'
                    : '🔎 Visual Inspection',
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.sm),
          ],
          if (entry.isCommitted)
            FilledButton.icon(
              onPressed: null,
              icon: const Icon(Icons.check_circle_rounded),
              label: const Text('Committed to Vault'),
            )
          else if (entry.hasFailed)
            FilledButton.icon(
              onPressed: onRetry,
              icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh_rounded),
              label: const Text('🔄 Retry Resolution'),
            )
          else if ((entry.isResolved || entry.needsReview) &&
              catalogProduct != null)
            FilledButton.icon(
              onPressed: isSaving ? null : onAdd,
              icon: isSaving
                  ? const SizedBox.square(
                      dimension: 18,
                      child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2),
                    )
                  : Icon(
                      isOwned
                          ? Icons.add_circle_outline_rounded
                          : Icons.inventory_2_outlined,
                    ),
              label: Text(
                isSaving
                    ? 'Saving...'
                    : entry.needsReview
                    ? '🔎 Review & Add'
                    : isOwned
                    ? '➕ Add Another Copy'
                    : '➕ Add to Vault',
              ),
            )
          else if (entry.needsReview && hasMultipleMatches)
            FilledButton.icon(
              onPressed: onReviewMatches,
              icon: const Icon(Icons.content_copy_rounded),
              label: Text('🔎 Review $matchCount Matches'),
            )
          else if (entry.needsReview) ...[
            FilledButton.icon(
              onPressed: isSaving ? null : onFindExisting,
              icon: const Icon(Icons.link_rounded),
              label: const Text('🔗 Find Existing in Vault'),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.sm),
            OutlinedButton.icon(
              onPressed: isSaving ? null : onManualAdd,
              icon: const Icon(Icons.edit_note_rounded),
              label: const Text('➕ Add Manually'),
            ),
          ],
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  static String _statusLabel(AtlasScanLedgerStatus status) {
    return switch (status) {
      AtlasScanLedgerStatus.observed => 'Observed',
      AtlasScanLedgerStatus.queued => 'Queued',
      AtlasScanLedgerStatus.resolving => 'Resolving',
      AtlasScanLedgerStatus.resolved => 'Resolved',
      AtlasScanLedgerStatus.needsReview => 'Review',
      AtlasScanLedgerStatus.failed => 'Failed',
      AtlasScanLedgerStatus.committed => 'Committed',
    };
  }

  static String _authenticityLabel(dynamic status) {
    return switch (status.name) {
      'strongEvidence' => 'Strong Evidence',
      'developingEvidence' => 'Developing Evidence',
      'reviewRecommended' => 'Review Recommended',
      _ => 'Insufficient Evidence',
    };
  }

  static String _statusMessage(AtlasScanLedgerEntry entry) {
    return switch (entry.status) {
      AtlasScanLedgerStatus.observed => 'Stable evidence captured.',
      AtlasScanLedgerStatus.queued => 'Waiting for an available resolver slot.',
      AtlasScanLedgerStatus.resolving =>
        'Atlas is resolving this item in the background.',
      AtlasScanLedgerStatus.resolved =>
        'Ready for explicit Vault review and save.',
      AtlasScanLedgerStatus.needsReview =>
        'Atlas needs your confirmation before this item can be committed.',
      AtlasScanLedgerStatus.failed =>
        entry.failureMessage?.trim().isNotEmpty == true
            ? entry.failureMessage!.trim()
            : 'Atlas could not complete resolution.',
      AtlasScanLedgerStatus.committed =>
        'This physical haul item has been committed.',
    };
  }

  static IconData _iconForStatus(AtlasScanLedgerStatus status) {
    return switch (status) {
      AtlasScanLedgerStatus.observed => Icons.visibility_outlined,
      AtlasScanLedgerStatus.queued => Icons.schedule_rounded,
      AtlasScanLedgerStatus.resolving => Icons.sync_rounded,
      AtlasScanLedgerStatus.resolved => Icons.check_circle_outline_rounded,
      AtlasScanLedgerStatus.needsReview => Icons.fact_check_outlined,
      AtlasScanLedgerStatus.failed => Icons.error_outline_rounded,
      AtlasScanLedgerStatus.committed => Icons.inventory_rounded,
    };
  }

  static Color _accentForStatus(AtlasScanLedgerStatus status) {
    return switch (status) {
      AtlasScanLedgerStatus.observed => AtlasColors.textSecondary,
      AtlasScanLedgerStatus.queued => AtlasColors.warning,
      AtlasScanLedgerStatus.resolving => AtlasColors.intelligenceBlue,
      AtlasScanLedgerStatus.resolved => AtlasColors.success,
      AtlasScanLedgerStatus.needsReview => AtlasColors.warning,
      AtlasScanLedgerStatus.failed => AtlasColors.danger,
      AtlasScanLedgerStatus.committed => AtlasColors.success,
    };
  }
}

class _VaultItemPickerSheet extends StatefulWidget {
  const _VaultItemPickerSheet({
    required this.collectionName,
    required this.collectionPops,
    required this.scannedBarcode,
  });

  final String collectionName;
  final List<Pop> collectionPops;
  final String scannedBarcode;

  @override
  State<_VaultItemPickerSheet> createState() => _VaultItemPickerSheetState();
}

class _VaultItemPickerSheetState extends State<_VaultItemPickerSheet> {
  final TextEditingController _searchController = TextEditingController();
  String _query = '';

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _searchController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  List<Pop> get _matches {
    final String query = _query.trim().toLowerCase();

    final List<Pop> pops = widget.collectionPops
        .where((Pop pop) {
          if (query.isEmpty) {
            return true;
          }

          final List<String> values = <String>[
            pop.name,
            pop.number,
            pop.series,
            pop.variant,
            pop.retailExclusive,
            pop.eventSticker,
            pop.limitedEdition,
            pop.signature,
            pop.sourceUpc,
          ];

          return values.any(
            (String value) => value.toLowerCase().contains(query),
          );
        })
        .toList(growable: false);

    pops.sort((Pop a, Pop b) {
      final int nameCompare = a.name.toLowerCase().compareTo(
        b.name.toLowerCase(),
      );
      if (nameCompare != 0) {
        return nameCompare;
      }
      return a.number.toLowerCase().compareTo(b.number.toLowerCase());
    });

    return pops;
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final List<Pop> matches = _matches;

    return SafeArea(
      child: FractionallySizedBox(
        heightFactor: 0.84,
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
                AtlasSpacing.lg,
                AtlasSpacing.lg,
                AtlasSpacing.lg,
                AtlasSpacing.md,
              ),
              child: AtlasSectionHeader(
                title: '🔗 Find Existing in Vault',
                subtitle:
                    'Select the existing collectible that belongs to barcode ${widget.scannedBarcode}.',
                icon: Icons.link_rounded,
                accentColor: AtlasColors.intelligenceBlue,
                trailing: AtlasStatusChip(
                  label: widget.collectionName,
                  icon: Icons.inventory_2_outlined,
                  accentColor: AtlasColors.intelligenceBlue,
                ),
              ),
            ),
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: AtlasSpacing.lg),
              child: TextField(
                controller: _searchController,
                autofocus: true,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(
                  prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.search_rounded),
                  hintText: 'Search name, number, variant, sticker...',
                  border: OutlineInputBorder(),
                ),
                onChanged: (String value) {
                  setState(() {
                    _query = value;
                  });
                },
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.md),
            Expanded(
              child: matches.isEmpty
                  ? const Center(
                      child: Padding(
                        padding: EdgeInsets.all(AtlasSpacing.lg),
                        child: Text(
                          'No matching Vault items. Try a different search or use Add Manually.',
                          textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                        ),
                      ),
                    )
                  : ListView.separated(
                      keyboardDismissBehavior:
                          ScrollViewKeyboardDismissBehavior.onDrag,
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
                        AtlasSpacing.lg,
                        0,
                        AtlasSpacing.lg,
                        AtlasSpacing.lg,
                      ),
                      itemCount: matches.length,
                      separatorBuilder: (_, _) =>
                          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.sm),
                      itemBuilder: (BuildContext context, int index) {
                        final Pop pop = matches[index];
                        final String barcode = pop.sourceUpc.trim();
                        final bool hasDifferentBarcode =
                            barcode.isNotEmpty &&
                            barcode != widget.scannedBarcode;

                        final List<String> details = <String>[
                          if (pop.number.trim().isNotEmpty)
                            '#${pop.number.trim()}',
                          if (pop.variant.trim().isNotEmpty &&
                              pop.variant.trim().toLowerCase() != 'common')
                            pop.variant.trim(),
                          if (pop.retailExclusive.trim().isNotEmpty &&
                              pop.retailExclusive.trim().toLowerCase() !=
                                  'none')
                            pop.retailExclusive.trim(),
                          if (pop.eventSticker.trim().isNotEmpty &&
                              pop.eventSticker.trim().toLowerCase() != 'none')
                            pop.eventSticker.trim(),
                          'Qty ${pop.quantity}',
                        ];

                        return AtlasGlassCard(
                          accentColor: hasDifferentBarcode
                              ? AtlasColors.warning
                              : AtlasColors.intelligenceBlue,
                          onTap: hasDifferentBarcode
                              ? null
                              : () {
                                  Navigator.pop(context, pop);
                                },
                          child: Row(
                            children: [
                              Icon(
                                hasDifferentBarcode
                                    ? Icons.warning_amber_rounded
                                    : Icons.inventory_2_outlined,
                                color: hasDifferentBarcode
                                    ? AtlasColors.warning
                                    : AtlasColors.intelligenceBlue,
                              ),
                              const SizedBox(width: AtlasSpacing.md),
                              Expanded(
                                child: Column(
                                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                  children: [
                                    Text(
                                      pop.name,
                                      style: Theme.of(context)
                                          .textTheme
                                          .titleSmall
                                          ?.copyWith(
                                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                                          ),
                                    ),
                                    if (details.isNotEmpty) ...[
                                      const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.xs),
                                      Text(
                                        details.join(' • '),
                                        style: Theme.of(context)
                                            .textTheme
                                            .bodySmall
                                            ?.copyWith(
                                              color: AtlasColors.textSecondary,
                                            ),
                                      ),
                                    ],
                                    if (hasDifferentBarcode) ...[
                                      const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.xs),
                                      Text(
                                        'Already linked to $barcode',
                                        style: Theme.of(context)
                                            .textTheme
                                            .bodySmall
                                            ?.copyWith(
                                              color: AtlasColors.warning,
                                            ),
                                      ),
                                    ],
                                  ],
                                ),
                              ),
                              if (!hasDifferentBarcode)
                                const Icon(Icons.chevron_right_rounded),
                            ],
                          ),
                        );
                      },
                    ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'dart:io';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../atlas/scan/acquisition/atlas_guided_inspection_capture_plan.dart';
import '../../atlas/scan/acquisition/atlas_inspection_image_capture_service.dart';
import '../../atlas/scan/authenticity/atlas_visual_inspection_service.dart';
import '../../atlas/scan/authenticity/models/atlas_visual_inspection.dart';
import '../../atlas/scan/authenticity/providers/atlas_authentication_evidence_interpreter.dart';

import '../../atlas/scan/models/scan_session.dart';

import '../../widgets/scanner/atlas_inspection_intelligence_dashboard.dart';

class AtlasVisualInspectionScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const AtlasVisualInspectionScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.session,
    this.imageCapture,
    this.textRecognition,
    this.inspectionService = const AtlasVisualInspectionService(),
  });

  final ScanSession session;
  final AtlasInspectionImageCapture? imageCapture;
  final AtlasTextRecognition? textRecognition;
  final AtlasVisualInspectionService inspectionService;

  @override
  State<AtlasVisualInspectionScreen> createState() =>
      _AtlasVisualInspectionScreenState();
}

class _AtlasVisualInspectionScreenState
    extends State<AtlasVisualInspectionScreen> {
  final _sticker = TextEditingController();
  final _stamp = TextEditingController();
  final _copyright = TextEditingController();
  final _signature = TextEditingController();
  final _provider = TextEditingController();
  final _number = TextEditingController();
  final _certificationText = TextEditingController();
  final _notes = TextEditingController();

  late final AtlasInspectionImageCapture _capture;
  late final AtlasTextRecognition _recognizer;

  final List<ScanImage> _images = <ScanImage>[];
  bool? _typography;
  bool? _border;
  bool? _printClear;
  bool? _cutEven;
  bool? _autograph;
  bool? _coa;
  bool _busy = false;
  ScanSession? _preview;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _capture = widget.imageCapture ?? AtlasInspectionImageCaptureService();
    _recognizer = widget.textRecognition ?? AtlasTextRecognitionService();

    final existing = widget.session.visualInspectionContribution?.inspection;
    if (existing != null) {
      _sticker.text = existing.stickerText;
      _stamp.text = existing.productionStampText;
      _copyright.text = existing.copyrightText;
      _signature.text = existing.signature;
      _provider.text = existing.authenticationProvider;
      _number.text = existing.authenticityNumber;
      _certificationText.text = existing.certificationText;
      _notes.text = existing.notes;
      _images.addAll(existing.images);
      _typography = existing.typographyConsistent;
      _border = existing.borderConsistent;
      _printClear = existing.stickerPrintClear;
      _cutEven = existing.stickerCutEven;
      _autograph = existing.hasAutograph;
      _coa = existing.hasCoa;
    }

    if (widget.session.authenticityContribution != null) {
      _preview = widget.session;
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _sticker.dispose();
    _stamp.dispose();
    _copyright.dispose();
    _signature.dispose();
    _provider.dispose();
    _number.dispose();
    _certificationText.dispose();
    _notes.dispose();
    _recognizer.close();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _captureStep(
    AtlasGuidedInspectionStep step, {
    TextEditingController? textTarget,
  }) async {
    final source = await showModalBottomSheet<AtlasInspectionImageSource>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => SafeArea(
        child: Wrap(
          children: [
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.photo_camera_outlined),
              title: const Text('Camera'),
              onTap: () =>
                  Navigator.pop(context, AtlasInspectionImageSource.camera),
            ),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.photo_library_outlined),
              title: const Text('Gallery'),
              onTap: () =>
                  Navigator.pop(context, AtlasInspectionImageSource.gallery),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );

    if (source == null) return;

    setState(() => _busy = true);
    try {
      final ScanImage? image = await _capture.capture(
        source: source,
        type: step.imageType,
      );
      if (image == null) return;

      String recognizedText = '';
      if (step.supportsTextRecognition &&
          textTarget != null &&
          image.hasLocalPath) {
        recognizedText = await _recognizer.recognize(image.localPath!);
      }

      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _images.removeWhere((ScanImage item) => item.type == step.imageType);
        _images.add(image);
        _preview = null;

        if (recognizedText.isNotEmpty && textTarget != null) {
          textTarget.text = recognizedText;
          if (step == AtlasGuidedInspectionStep.coa) {
            final interpretation =
                AtlasAuthenticationEvidenceInterpreter.interpret(
                  provider: _provider.text,
                  certificateNumber: _number.text,
                  certificationText: recognizedText,
                  hasCoa: _coa,
                );
            if (_provider.text.trim().isEmpty &&
                interpretation.detectedProviderDisplayName.isNotEmpty) {
              _provider.text = interpretation.detectedProviderDisplayName;
            }
            if (_number.text.trim().isEmpty &&
                interpretation.detectedCertificateNumber.isNotEmpty) {
              _number.text = interpretation.detectedCertificateNumber;
            }
          }
        }
      });
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);
    }
  }

  AtlasVisualInspection _inspection() => AtlasVisualInspection(
    stickerText: _sticker.text,
    productionStampText: _stamp.text,
    copyrightText: _copyright.text,
    signature: _signature.text,
    authenticationProvider: _provider.text,
    authenticityNumber: _number.text,
    certificationText: _certificationText.text,
    typographyConsistent: _typography,
    borderConsistent: _border,
    stickerPrintClear: _printClear,
    stickerCutEven: _cutEven,
    hasAutograph: _autograph,
    hasCoa: _coa,
    notes: _notes.text,
    images: _images,
  );

  Future<void> _analyze({required bool finish}) async {
    setState(() => _busy = true);
    try {
      final ScanSession session = await widget.inspectionService.inspect(
        session: widget.session,
        inspection: _inspection(),
      );

      if (!mounted) return;
      if (finish) {
        Navigator.pop(context, session);
      } else {
        setState(() => _preview = session);
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);
    }
  }

  bool _hasImage(AtlasGuidedInspectionStep step) {
    return _images.any((ScanImage image) => image.type == step.imageType);
  }

  ScanSession get _displaySession => _preview ?? widget.session;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final List<ScanImage> localPreviewImages = _images
        .where((ScanImage image) => image.hasLocalPath)
        .toList(growable: false);
    final AtlasVisualInspection draft = _inspection();

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('Visual Inspection')),
      body: VaultScrollView(
        children: [
          _inspectionHero(draft),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.lg),
          const AtlasInfoBanner(
            title: 'Evidence, not certification',
            message:
                'Atlas records inspection evidence and warning signals. It does not certify an item as authentic or fake.',
            icon: Icons.info_outline_rounded,
            accentColor: AtlasColors.intelligenceBlue,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.lg),
          _captureOnlySection(AtlasGuidedInspectionStep.frontBox),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.lg),
          _textCaptureSection(
            step: AtlasGuidedInspectionStep.stickerWindow,
            controller: _sticker,
            label: 'Sticker Text',
            checks: [
              _check(
                'Print is clear',
                _printClear,
                (bool? value) => _printClear = value,
              ),
              _check(
                'Cut and border are even',
                _cutEven,
                (bool? value) => _cutEven = value,
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.lg),
          _textCaptureSection(
            step: AtlasGuidedInspectionStep.bottomStamp,
            controller: _stamp,
            label: 'Production Code (JJL/FAC)',
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.lg),
          _packagingSection(),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.lg),
          _autographSection(),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.lg),
          _coaSection(),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.lg),
          AtlasFormField(
            controller: _notes,
            label: 'Inspection Notes',
            icon: Icons.notes_rounded,
            maxLines: 4,
            onChanged: (_) => setState(() => _preview = null),
          ),
          if (localPreviewImages.isNotEmpty) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.lg),
            _evidenceStrip(localPreviewImages),
          ],
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.xl),
          const AtlasSectionHeader(
            title: 'Atlas Inspection Intelligence',
            subtitle:
                'Five separate signals keep evidence quality, reference strength, concern, variant state, and autograph documentation distinct.',
            icon: Icons.auto_awesome_rounded,
            accentColor: AtlasColors.intelligenceBlue,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.md),
          AtlasInspectionIntelligenceDashboard(
            session: _displaySession,
            inspection: draft,
          ),
          if (_preview?.authenticityContribution != null) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.lg),
            AtlasAuthenticityCard(
              assessment: _preview!.authenticityContribution!.assessment,
            ),
          ],
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.xl),
          OutlinedButton.icon(
            onPressed: _busy ? null : () => _analyze(finish: false),
            icon: const Icon(Icons.analytics_outlined),
            label: const Text('🧠 Preview Assessment'),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.sm),
          FilledButton.icon(
            onPressed: _busy ? null : () => _analyze(finish: true),
            icon: _busy
                ? const SizedBox.square(
                    dimension: 18,
                    child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2),
                  )
                : const Icon(Icons.check_rounded),
            label: const Text('Save Inspection'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _inspectionHero(AtlasVisualInspection draft) {
    final int captured = AtlasGuidedInspectionCapturePlan.steps
        .where(_hasImage)
        .length;
    final int inspected = draft.inspectedAreas.length;
    final double progress =
        inspected / AtlasGuidedInspectionCapturePlan.steps.length;

    return AtlasGlassCard(
      accentColor: AtlasColors.intelligenceBlue,
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          AtlasSectionHeader(
            title: '🔎 Guided Visual Inspection',
            subtitle:
                'Capture only the evidence available for this item. Missing evidence is never treated as counterfeit evidence.',
            icon: Icons.document_scanner_outlined,
            accentColor: AtlasColors.intelligenceBlue,
            trailing: AtlasStatusChip(
              label: '$inspected / 6 inspected',
              icon: Icons.fact_check_outlined,
              accentColor: AtlasColors.intelligenceBlue,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.md),
          ClipRRect(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(999),
            child: LinearProgressIndicator(
              value: progress.clamp(0.0, 1.0).toDouble(),
              minHeight: 10,
              backgroundColor: AtlasColors.border,
              valueColor: const AlwaysStoppedAnimation<Color>(
                AtlasColors.intelligenceBlue,
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.sm),
          Text(
            '$captured of 6 guided images captured',
            style: AtlasTypography.bodySmall,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.md),
          Wrap(
            spacing: AtlasSpacing.sm,
            runSpacing: AtlasSpacing.sm,
            children: AtlasGuidedInspectionCapturePlan.steps
                .map((step) => _stepChip(step))
                .toList(growable: false),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _stepChip(AtlasGuidedInspectionStep step) {
    final _InspectionStepState state = _stepState(step);
    return AtlasStatusChip(
      label: '${_shortStepTitle(step)} • ${state.label}',
      icon: state.icon,
      accentColor: state.color,
    );
  }

  _InspectionStepState _stepState(AtlasGuidedInspectionStep step) {
    final assessment = _preview?.authenticityContribution?.assessment;
    final bool hasDiscrepancy =
        assessment?.evaluatedSignals.any(
          (signal) =>
              signal.area == step.inspectionArea && signal.isDiscrepancy,
        ) ??
        false;

    if (hasDiscrepancy) {
      return const _InspectionStepState(
        'Needs attention',
        Icons.warning_amber_rounded,
        AtlasColors.warning,
      );
    }

    final bool hasArea = _inspection().inspectedAreas.contains(
      step.inspectionArea,
    );
    if (_preview != null && hasArea) {
      return const _InspectionStepState(
        'Reviewed',
        Icons.task_alt_rounded,
        AtlasColors.success,
      );
    }
    if (hasArea || _hasImage(step)) {
      return const _InspectionStepState(
        'Captured',
        Icons.photo_camera_back_outlined,
        AtlasColors.intelligenceBlue,
      );
    }
    return const _InspectionStepState(
      'Not inspected',
      Icons.radio_button_unchecked_rounded,
      AtlasColors.textSecondary,
    );
  }

  Widget _captureOnlySection(AtlasGuidedInspectionStep step) {
    return AtlasExpandableSection(
      title: step.title,
      subtitle: step.guidance,
      icon: Icons.center_focus_strong_rounded,
      badge: _sectionBadge(step),
      accentColor: _stepState(step).color,
      initiallyExpanded: true,
      child: _captureButton(step),
    );
  }

  Widget _textCaptureSection({
    required AtlasGuidedInspectionStep step,
    required TextEditingController controller,
    required String label,
    List<Widget> checks = const <Widget>[],
  }) {
    return AtlasExpandableSection(
      title: step.title,
      subtitle: step.guidance,
      icon: Icons.center_focus_strong_rounded,
      badge: _sectionBadge(step),
      accentColor: _stepState(step).color,
      initiallyExpanded: true,
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          AtlasFormField(
            controller: controller,
            label: label,
            icon: Icons.text_snippet_outlined,
            maxLines: 3,
            onChanged: (_) => setState(() => _preview = null),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.sm),
          _captureButton(step, textTarget: controller),
          ...checks,
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _packagingSection() {
    final step = AtlasGuidedInspectionStep.packagingCopyright;
    return AtlasExpandableSection(
      title: step.title,
      subtitle: step.guidance,
      icon: Icons.text_fields_rounded,
      badge: _sectionBadge(step),
      accentColor: _stepState(step).color,
      initiallyExpanded: true,
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          AtlasFormField(
            controller: _copyright,
            label: 'Copyright Text / Year',
            icon: Icons.copyright_rounded,
            maxLines: 3,
            onChanged: (_) => setState(() => _preview = null),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.sm),
          _captureButton(step, textTarget: _copyright),
          _check(
            'Typography looks consistent',
            _typography,
            (bool? value) => _typography = value,
          ),
          _check(
            'Box borders look consistent',
            _border,
            (bool? value) => _border = value,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _autographSection() {
    final step = AtlasGuidedInspectionStep.autograph;
    return AtlasExpandableSection(
      title: step.title,
      subtitle: step.guidance,
      icon: Icons.draw_outlined,
      badge: _sectionBadge(step),
      accentColor: _stepState(step).color,
      initiallyExpanded: true,
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          _check(
            'Autograph is present',
            _autograph,
            (bool? value) => _autograph = value,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.sm),
          _captureButton(step),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.md),
          AtlasFormField(
            controller: _signature,
            label: 'Signature / Signer',
            icon: Icons.draw_outlined,
            onChanged: (_) => setState(() => _preview = null),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _coaSection() {
    final step = AtlasGuidedInspectionStep.coa;
    return AtlasExpandableSection(
      title: step.title,
      subtitle: step.guidance,
      icon: Icons.workspace_premium_outlined,
      badge: _sectionBadge(step),
      accentColor: _stepState(step).color,
      initiallyExpanded: true,
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          _check('COA is present', _coa, (bool? value) => _coa = value),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.sm),
          _captureButton(step, textTarget: _certificationText),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.md),
          AtlasFormField(
            controller: _provider,
            label: 'Authentication Provider',
            icon: Icons.verified_outlined,
            onChanged: (_) => setState(() => _preview = null),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.md),
          AtlasFormField(
            controller: _number,
            label: 'Authenticity Number',
            icon: Icons.numbers_rounded,
            onChanged: (_) => setState(() => _preview = null),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.md),
          AtlasFormField(
            controller: _certificationText,
            label: 'Observed Certification Text',
            icon: Icons.document_scanner_outlined,
            maxLines: 4,
            onChanged: (_) => setState(() => _preview = null),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.sm),
          AtlasGlassPanel(
            accentColor: AtlasColors.intelligenceBlue,
            child: Text(
              'OCR text is observational. Provider names and certificate numbers are not externally verified by Atlas.',
              style: AtlasTypography.bodySmall,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _sectionBadge(AtlasGuidedInspectionStep step) {
    final state = _stepState(step);
    return AtlasExpandableBadge(
      label: state.label,
      icon: state.icon,
      color: state.color,
    );
  }

  Widget _captureButton(
    AtlasGuidedInspectionStep step, {
    TextEditingController? textTarget,
  }) {
    final bool captured = _hasImage(step);
    return OutlinedButton.icon(
      onPressed: _busy
          ? null
          : () => _captureStep(step, textTarget: textTarget),
      icon: Icon(
        captured ? Icons.check_circle_outline : Icons.add_a_photo_outlined,
      ),
      label: Text(
        captured ? 'Captured — Replace Image' : 'Capture or Choose Image',
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _check(String label, bool? value, ValueChanged<bool?> update) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: AtlasSpacing.sm),
      child: DropdownButtonFormField<bool?>(
        initialValue: value,
        decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: label),
        items: const [
          DropdownMenuItem<bool?>(value: null, child: Text('Not inspected')),
          DropdownMenuItem<bool?>(value: true, child: Text('Yes')),
          DropdownMenuItem<bool?>(
            value: false,
            child: Text('No / warning seen'),
          ),
        ],
        onChanged: (bool? next) => setState(() {
          update(next);
          _preview = null;
        }),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _evidenceStrip(List<ScanImage> images) {
    return AtlasGlassCard(
      accentColor: AtlasColors.intelligenceBlue,
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          const AtlasSectionHeader(
            title: '📸 Captured Evidence',
            subtitle: 'Local previews for the current inspection session.',
            icon: Icons.photo_library_outlined,
            accentColor: AtlasColors.intelligenceBlue,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.md),
          SizedBox(
            height: 104,
            child: ListView.separated(
              scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
              itemCount: images.length,
              separatorBuilder: (_, _) =>
                  const SizedBox(width: AtlasSpacing.sm),
              itemBuilder: (_, int index) => ClipRRect(
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                child: Image.file(
                  File(images[index].localPath!),
                  width: 92,
                  fit: BoxFit.cover,
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  String _shortStepTitle(AtlasGuidedInspectionStep step) {
    return switch (step) {
      AtlasGuidedInspectionStep.frontBox => 'Front',
      AtlasGuidedInspectionStep.stickerWindow => 'Sticker',
      AtlasGuidedInspectionStep.bottomStamp => 'Stamp',
      AtlasGuidedInspectionStep.packagingCopyright => 'Packaging',
      AtlasGuidedInspectionStep.autograph => 'Autograph',
      AtlasGuidedInspectionStep.coa => 'COA',
    };
  }
}

class _InspectionStepState {
  const _InspectionStepState(this.label, this.icon, this.color);

  final String label;
  final IconData icon;
  final Color color;
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../atlas/scan/authenticity/models/atlas_authenticity_assessment.dart';
import '../../theme/atlas_colors.dart';
import '../../theme/atlas_spacing.dart';
import '../../theme/atlas_typography.dart';
import '../atlas/atlas_glass_card.dart';
import '../atlas/atlas_glass_panel.dart';
import '../atlas/components/atlas_section_header.dart';
import '../atlas/components/atlas_status_chip.dart';

class AtlasAuthenticityCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const AtlasAuthenticityCard({super.key, required this.assessment});

  final AtlasAuthenticityAssessment assessment;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final _ConcernPresentation concern = _concernPresentation(
      assessment.concernLevel,
    );

    return AtlasGlassCard(
      accentColor: concern.color,
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          AtlasSectionHeader(
            title: '🧠 Inspection Assessment',
            subtitle: assessment.summary,
            icon: Icons.analytics_outlined,
            accentColor: concern.color,
            trailing: AtlasStatusChip(
              label: concern.label,
              icon: Icons.shield_outlined,
              accentColor: concern.color,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.md),
          Wrap(
            spacing: AtlasSpacing.sm,
            runSpacing: AtlasSpacing.sm,
            children: [
              _MetricPanel(
                label: '📷 Evidence',
                value: assessment.evidenceConfidence,
                color: AtlasColors.intelligenceBlue,
              ),
              _MetricPanel(
                label: '📚 Reference',
                value: assessment.referenceCoverage,
                color: AtlasColors.vaultGold,
              ),
              _MetricPanel(
                label: '🛡️ Concern',
                value: assessment.aggregatedConcernScore,
                color: concern.color,
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.sm),
          Text(
            'Inspection coverage: ${(assessment.inspectionCoverage * 100).round().clamp(0, 100)}%',
            style: AtlasTypography.bodySmall.copyWith(
              color: AtlasColors.textSecondary,
            ),
          ),
          if (assessment.signals.isNotEmpty) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.md),
            ...assessment.signals.map(
              (signal) => Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: AtlasSpacing.sm),
                child: AtlasGlassPanel(
                  accentColor: signal.isWarning
                      ? AtlasColors.warning
                      : AtlasColors.intelligenceBlue,
                  child: Text(
                    '${signal.title}: ${signal.message}',
                    style: AtlasTypography.bodySmall,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.sm),
          Text(
            'Atlas reports evidence and warning signals. This assessment is not a certification or a definitive authentic/fake verdict.',
            style: AtlasTypography.bodySmall.copyWith(
              color: AtlasColors.textMuted,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _MetricPanel extends StatelessWidget {
  const _MetricPanel({
    required this.label,
    required this.value,
    required this.color,
  });

  final String label;
  final double value;
  final Color color;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return SizedBox(
      width: 132,
      child: AtlasGlassPanel(
        accentColor: color,
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Text(
              label,
              style: AtlasTypography.bodySmall.copyWith(
                color: AtlasColors.textSecondary,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.xs),
            Text(
              '${(value * 100).round().clamp(0, 100)}%',
              style: AtlasTypography.metricSmall.copyWith(color: color),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _ConcernPresentation {
  const _ConcernPresentation(this.label, this.color);

  final String label;
  final Color color;
}

_ConcernPresentation _concernPresentation(AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel level) {
  return switch (level) {
    AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.noObviousConcerns =>
      const _ConcernPresentation('No obvious concerns', AtlasColors.success),
    AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.reviewRecommended =>
      const _ConcernPresentation('Review recommended', AtlasColors.warning),
    AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.significantInconsistencies =>
      const _ConcernPresentation(
        'Significant inconsistencies',
        AtlasColors.warning,
      ),
    AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.strongCounterfeitIndicators =>
      const _ConcernPresentation(
        'Strong warning indicators',
        AtlasColors.danger,
      ),
    AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.insufficientData =>
      const _ConcernPresentation(
        'Insufficient data',
        AtlasColors.textSecondary,
      ),
  };
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../atlas/scan/authenticity/models/atlas_authenticity_assessment.dart';
import '../../atlas/scan/authenticity/models/atlas_visual_inspection.dart';
import '../../atlas/scan/models/scan_session.dart';
import '../../atlas/scan/results/scan_variant_status.dart';
import '../../theme/atlas_colors.dart';
import '../../theme/atlas_spacing.dart';
import '../../theme/atlas_typography.dart';
import '../atlas/atlas_glass_card.dart';
import '../atlas/atlas_glass_panel.dart';
import '../atlas/components/atlas_section_header.dart';
import '../atlas/components/atlas_status_chip.dart';

class AtlasInspectionIntelligenceDashboard extends StatelessWidget {
  const AtlasInspectionIntelligenceDashboard({
    super.key,
    required this.session,
    required this.inspection,
  });

  final ScanSession session;
  final AtlasVisualInspection inspection;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final AtlasAuthenticityAssessment? assessment =
        session.authenticityContribution?.assessment;

    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
      children: [
        _EvidenceConfidenceCard(
          value:
              assessment?.evidenceConfidence ??
              inspection.resolvedEvidenceConfidence,
          hasAssessment: assessment != null,
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.md),
        _ReferenceCoverageCard(assessment: assessment),
        const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.md),
        _AuthenticityConcernCard(assessment: assessment),
        const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.md),
        _VariantConfirmationCard(resolution: session.variantResolution),
        const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.md),
        _AutographCoaEvidenceCard(inspection: inspection),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _EvidenceConfidenceCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const _EvidenceConfidenceCard({
    required this.value,
    required this.hasAssessment,
  });

  final double value;
  final bool hasAssessment;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final int percent = (value * 100).round().clamp(0, 100).toInt();
    final String label = !hasAssessment
        ? 'Draft evidence'
        : value >= 0.80
        ? 'Clear evidence'
        : value >= 0.55
        ? 'Usable evidence'
        : value >= 0.40
        ? 'Limited evidence'
        : 'Insufficient evidence';

    return _MetricCard(
      title: '📷 Evidence Confidence',
      subtitle: 'How clearly Atlas can observe the evidence you supplied.',
      icon: Icons.center_focus_strong_rounded,
      accentColor: AtlasColors.intelligenceBlue,
      value: '$percent%',
      statusLabel: label,
      progress: value,
    );
  }
}

class _ReferenceCoverageCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const _ReferenceCoverageCard({required this.assessment});

  final AtlasAuthenticityAssessment? assessment;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final double? value = assessment?.referenceCoverage;
    final int percent = value == null
        ? 0
        : (value * 100).round().clamp(0, 100).toInt();
    final String label = value == null
        ? 'Run preview'
        : value >= 0.75
        ? 'Strong reference coverage'
        : value >= 0.50
        ? 'Useful reference coverage'
        : 'Limited reference coverage';

    return _MetricCard(
      title: '📚 Reference Coverage',
      subtitle:
          'How complete and trustworthy the comparison reference is for this item.',
      icon: Icons.menu_book_rounded,
      accentColor: AtlasColors.vaultGold,
      value: value == null ? '—' : '$percent%',
      statusLabel: label,
      progress: value,
    );
  }
}

class _AuthenticityConcernCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const _AuthenticityConcernCard({required this.assessment});

  final AtlasAuthenticityAssessment? assessment;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (assessment == null) {
      return const _MetricCard(
        title: '🛡️ Authenticity Concern',
        subtitle:
            'Atlas reports warning signals and inconsistencies, never a definitive authentic/fake verdict.',
        icon: Icons.shield_outlined,
        accentColor: AtlasColors.textSecondary,
        value: '—',
        statusLabel: 'Run preview',
      );
    }

    final _ConcernPresentation presentation = _concernPresentation(
      assessment!.concernLevel,
    );
    final int percent = (assessment!.aggregatedConcernScore * 100)
        .round()
        .clamp(0, 100)
        .toInt();

    return _MetricCard(
      title: '🛡️ Authenticity Concern',
      subtitle: assessment!.summary,
      icon: Icons.shield_outlined,
      accentColor: presentation.color,
      value: '$percent%',
      statusLabel: presentation.label,
      progress: assessment!.aggregatedConcernScore,
    );
  }
}

class _VariantConfirmationCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const _VariantConfirmationCard({required this.resolution});

  final ScanVariantResolution? resolution;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final _VariantPresentation presentation = _variantPresentation(resolution);

    return AtlasGlassCard(
      accentColor: presentation.color,
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          AtlasSectionHeader(
            title: '🧩 Variant Confirmation',
            subtitle:
                'Visual evidence may recommend review, but it never silently rewrites the catalog-resolved identity.',
            icon: Icons.extension_rounded,
            accentColor: presentation.color,
            trailing: AtlasStatusChip(
              label: presentation.label,
              icon: presentation.icon,
              accentColor: presentation.color,
            ),
          ),
          if (resolution != null) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.md),
            AtlasGlassPanel(
              accentColor: presentation.color,
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text(
                    '${(resolution!.confidence * 100).round().clamp(0, 100)}% variant confidence',
                    style: AtlasTypography.metricSmall,
                  ),
                  if (resolution!.confirmedTraits.isNotEmpty) ...[
                    const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.sm),
                    Text(
                      'Confirmed: ${resolution!.confirmedTraits.join(', ')}',
                      style: AtlasTypography.bodySmall,
                    ),
                  ],
                  if (resolution!.unconfirmedTraits.isNotEmpty) ...[
                    const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.xs),
                    Text(
                      'Needs review: ${resolution!.unconfirmedTraits.join(', ')}',
                      style: AtlasTypography.bodySmall.copyWith(
                        color: AtlasColors.warning,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                  if (resolution!.candidateProductIds.length > 1) ...[
                    const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.xs),
                    Text(
                      '${resolution!.candidateProductIds.length} catalog candidates remain.',
                      style: AtlasTypography.bodySmall,
                    ),
                  ],
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _AutographCoaEvidenceCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const _AutographCoaEvidenceCard({required this.inspection});

  final AtlasVisualInspection inspection;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final bool autographObserved =
        inspection.hasAutograph == true ||
        inspection.signature.trim().isNotEmpty;
    final bool coaObserved =
        inspection.hasCoa == true ||
        inspection.authenticationProvider.trim().isNotEmpty ||
        inspection.authenticityNumber.trim().isNotEmpty ||
        inspection.certificationText.trim().isNotEmpty;
    final bool hasAnyEvidence = autographObserved || coaObserved;

    final Color color = hasAnyEvidence
        ? AtlasColors.intelligenceBlue
        : AtlasColors.textSecondary;

    return AtlasGlassCard(
      accentColor: color,
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          AtlasSectionHeader(
            title: '✍️ Autograph / 📜 COA Evidence',
            subtitle:
                'Observed documentation is recorded separately from external provider verification.',
            icon: Icons.draw_outlined,
            accentColor: color,
            trailing: AtlasStatusChip(
              label: hasAnyEvidence ? 'Observed' : 'Not inspected',
              icon: hasAnyEvidence
                  ? Icons.visibility_outlined
                  : Icons.remove_red_eye_outlined,
              accentColor: color,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.md),
          AtlasGlassPanel(
            accentColor: color,
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                _EvidenceRow(
                  label: 'Autograph',
                  value: autographObserved
                      ? (inspection.signature.trim().isEmpty
                            ? 'Present / observed'
                            : inspection.signature.trim())
                      : inspection.hasAutograph == false
                      ? 'Marked not present'
                      : 'Not inspected',
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.sm),
                _EvidenceRow(
                  label: 'COA',
                  value: coaObserved
                      ? 'Documentation observed'
                      : inspection.hasCoa == false
                      ? 'Marked not present'
                      : 'Not inspected',
                ),
                if (inspection.authenticationProvider.trim().isNotEmpty) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.sm),
                  _EvidenceRow(
                    label: 'Provider',
                    value: inspection.authenticationProvider.trim(),
                  ),
                ],
                if (inspection.authenticityNumber.trim().isNotEmpty) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.sm),
                  _EvidenceRow(
                    label: 'Certificate',
                    value: inspection.authenticityNumber.trim(),
                  ),
                ],
                if (hasAnyEvidence) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.md),
                  Text(
                    'Provider verification: not performed by Atlas in Scanner 5.',
                    style: AtlasTypography.bodySmall.copyWith(
                      color: AtlasColors.textSecondary,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _MetricCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const _MetricCard({
    required this.title,
    required this.subtitle,
    required this.icon,
    required this.accentColor,
    required this.value,
    required this.statusLabel,
    this.progress,
  });

  final String title;
  final String subtitle;
  final IconData icon;
  final Color accentColor;
  final String value;
  final String statusLabel;
  final double? progress;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AtlasGlassCard(
      accentColor: accentColor,
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          AtlasSectionHeader(
            title: title,
            subtitle: subtitle,
            icon: icon,
            accentColor: accentColor,
            trailing: AtlasStatusChip(
              label: statusLabel,
              icon: icon,
              accentColor: accentColor,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: AtlasSpacing.md),
          Row(
            children: [
              Text(
                value,
                style: AtlasTypography.metricMedium.copyWith(
                  color: accentColor,
                ),
              ),
              if (progress != null) ...[
                const SizedBox(width: AtlasSpacing.lg),
                Expanded(
                  child: ClipRRect(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(999),
                    child: LinearProgressIndicator(
                      value: progress!.clamp(0.0, 1.0).toDouble(),
                      minHeight: 8,
                      backgroundColor: AtlasColors.border,
                      valueColor: AlwaysStoppedAnimation<Color>(accentColor),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _EvidenceRow extends StatelessWidget {
  const _EvidenceRow({required this.label, required this.value});

  final String label;
  final String value;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Row(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        SizedBox(width: 88, child: Text(label, style: AtlasTypography.label)),
        const SizedBox(width: AtlasSpacing.sm),
        Expanded(child: Text(value, style: AtlasTypography.bodyMedium)),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _ConcernPresentation {
  const _ConcernPresentation(this.label, this.color);

  final String label;
  final Color color;
}

_ConcernPresentation _concernPresentation(AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel level) {
  return switch (level) {
    AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.noObviousConcerns =>
      const _ConcernPresentation('No obvious concerns', AtlasColors.success),
    AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.reviewRecommended =>
      const _ConcernPresentation('Review recommended', AtlasColors.warning),
    AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.significantInconsistencies =>
      const _ConcernPresentation(
        'Significant inconsistencies',
        AtlasColors.warning,
      ),
    AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.strongCounterfeitIndicators =>
      const _ConcernPresentation(
        'Strong warning indicators',
        AtlasColors.danger,
      ),
    AtlasAuthenticityConcernLevel.insufficientData =>
      const _ConcernPresentation(
        'Insufficient data',
        AtlasColors.textSecondary,
      ),
  };
}

class _VariantPresentation {
  const _VariantPresentation(this.label, this.color, this.icon);

  final String label;
  final Color color;
  final IconData icon;
}

_VariantPresentation _variantPresentation(ScanVariantResolution? resolution) {
  if (resolution == null) {
    return const _VariantPresentation(
      'Not evaluated',
      AtlasColors.textSecondary,
      Icons.help_outline_rounded,
    );
  }

  return switch (resolution.status) {
    ScanVariantStatus.exactMatch => const _VariantPresentation(
      'Confirmed',
      AtlasColors.success,
      Icons.check_circle_outline_rounded,
    ),
    ScanVariantStatus.likelyMatch => const _VariantPresentation(
      'Likely match',
      AtlasColors.intelligenceBlue,
      Icons.auto_awesome_rounded,
    ),
    ScanVariantStatus.multipleCandidates => const _VariantPresentation(
      'Choose variant',
      AtlasColors.warning,
      Icons.rule_rounded,
    ),
    ScanVariantStatus.visualConfirmationRequired => const _VariantPresentation(
      'Visual confirmation needed',
      AtlasColors.warning,
      Icons.visibility_outlined,
    ),
    ScanVariantStatus.unknown => const _VariantPresentation(
      'Unknown',
      AtlasColors.textSecondary,
      Icons.help_outline_rounded,
    ),
  };
}
